*Rebecca Ramnauth,Brian Scassellati*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了Transformer模型适配位置（早期、中期、晚期层或全栈）对学习效果的影响，发现不同目标任务（如词汇绑定、事实关联、行为学习、因果推理和程序推理）具有独特的"适配几何"特征，即在不同层进行适配时在获取、迁移和边界性方面表现各异，且这些模式在多个模型家族中保持一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管实际应用中Transformer适配通常跨层分布，但适配位置如何影响模型学习的内容、泛化能力和选择性尚未被系统研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，将适配位置确立为控制模型学习行为的关键设计变量。

Method: 构建了一个包含五个目标任务（词汇绑定、事实关联、行为策略学习、因果映射和程序推理）的受控基准；为每个目标定义"适配几何"——在早期、中期、晚期层或全栈使用LoRA进行适配时的获取、迁移和边界性特征；通过参数匹配控制和跨五个模型家族的方向对比验证结果的普适性。

Result: 不同目标展现出不同的适配几何：词汇绑定在早期层获取和边界性表现最佳但迁移需更广更新；事实关联在晚期层表现更好；行为学习分离了晚期层的动作获取和中期层的策略门控；因果和程序推理从中层或全栈适配中获益最大；这些模式在参数匹配控制下基本保持，且大多数方向对比在五个模型家族中可复现。

Conclusion: 适配位置是影响模型学习、泛化和保持行为的关键设计变量，为精细化控制Transformer微调提供了新的设计维度。

Abstract: Transformer adaptation is typically distributed across model depth, even when the intended change is narrow. We investigate how adaptation site shapes what a model learns, how well that learning generalizes, and how selectively it is applied. We introduce a controlled benchmark spanning five objectives (lexical binding, factual association, behavioral policy learning, causal mapping, and procedural reasoning) and define each objective's "adaptation geometry" as its profile of acquisition, transfer, and boundedness under full-stack and early-, middle-, or late-layer LoRA. The objectives exhibit distinct geometries. Lexical binding favors early-layer adaptation for acquisition and boundedness but requires broader updates for transfer; factual association favors later layers among localized adapters; behavioral learning separates late-layer action acquisition from middle-layer policy gating; and causal and procedural transfer benefit most from middle- or full-stack adaptation. These patterns largely persist under parameter-matched controls, and most corresponding directional contrasts replicate across five model families. These findings establish adaptation site as a key design variable for controlling what models learn, generalize, and leave unchanged.

</details>


### [94] [OmniDelta: Skill-Driven Budget Allocation for Token Compression in OmniLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25669)
*Haoyang Huang,Wenjie Huang,Tianqi Xu,Hongyaoxing Gu,Kang Tan,Yikai Fu,Yuhao Shen,Tianyu Liu,Baolin Zhang,Jun Zhang,Xinyi Hu,Jun Dai,Shuang Ge,Lei Chen,Yue Li,Mingchen Wang,Meng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OmniDelta是一个无需训练的技能驱动框架，通过意图感知的模态间预算分配和内容感知的模态内预算分配，解决OmniLLM中长音频-视频token序列导致的高内存和推理代价问题，在保持总保留token比例不变的前提下，建立新的精度-效率帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OmniLLM处理长音频-视频token序列时存在高昂的内存和推理成本；现有压缩方法主要关注在固定预算下选择重要token，忽略了预算分配这一前置问题；直接利用查询-音频/视频相似度进行模态间预算分配不可靠，且统一的模态内预算可能遗漏关键证据或保留冗余内容。

Method: 提出OmniDelta框架，包含两个核心组件：1) 意图感知的模态间分配：构建音频和视频技能池，根据查询需求动态调整固定保留token预算在各模态间的分配；2) 内容感知的模态内分配：利用局部复杂度和时间冗余性，在音频片段和视频帧层面重新分配模态预算。最终局部预算可与现有剪枝策略结合，在保持总保留token比例不变的前提下改变预算分配位置。

Result: 在四个音频-视频基准测试上使用两种Qwen2.5-Omni模型进行评估，OmniDelta在所有剪枝比例下建立了新的精度-效率帕累托前沿。在Qwen2.5-Omni-7B上以25% token保留率运行时，GPU内存减少22.0%，端到端推理速度提升1.64倍。

Conclusion: OmniDelta通过解耦模态间和模态内的预算分配问题，提供了一种无需训练的高效框架，显著改善了OmniLLM在长序列场景下的推理效率与性能平衡。

Abstract: Emerging Omni-modal Large Language Models (OmniLLMs) enable unified understanding of text, audio, and video, but their long audio-video token sequences introduce substantial memory and inference costs. Existing compression methods mainly focus on selecting important tokens under fixed budgets, leaving the preceding budget-allocation problem underexplored. We show that direct query-to-audio/video similarity is unreliable for inter-modal budget allocation, and that uniform intra-modal budgets can miss key evidence while retaining redundant content. To address these limitations, we propose OmniDelta, a training-free, skill-driven framework that couples intent-aware inter-modal allocation with content-aware intra-modal allocation. OmniDelta first constructs audio and video skill pools to shift the fixed retained-token budget according to query demand, then reallocates modality budgets over audio segments and video frames using local complexity and temporal redundancy. The resulting local budgets can be combined with existing pruning strategies, preserving the total retained-token ratio while changing where the budget is spent. Experiments on four audio-video benchmarks with two Qwen2.5-Omni models show that OmniDelta establishes a new accuracy-efficiency Pareto frontier across pruning ratios. At 25% token retention on Qwen2.5-Omni-7B, OmniDelta reduces GPU memory by 22.0% and achieves a 1.64x end-to-end speedup over full-token inference.

</details>


### [95] [DecoEvo: Score-Decoupled Co-Evolution of Solver and Rubric-Generator Skills in Text Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25675)
*Jiangwang Chen,Zixin Song,Junlin Liu,Shuaiyu Zhou,Haiyan Wu,Haihan Shi,Chenxi Zhou,Hanqing Li,Xiao Yang,Da Zhu,Guanjun Jiang,Hai Wan,Xibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DecoEvo 提出解耦式共同进化框架，将求解器技能与评估标准（rubric）生成器技能分离优化，无需金牌标准即可协同进化，在五个基准测试中相比 SkillOpt 提升 2.8-5.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本空间优化方法在开放式任务中面临瓶颈：当求解器在评估标准衡量的维度上提升后，未覆盖的维度无法获得优化信号；而简单进化评估标准又可能因求解器评分导致虚假进步（标准变容易）。需要一种无需金牌标准、解耦目标的方法来协同进化求解器和评估标准。

Method: DecoEvo 将优化分为两个独立技能：求解器技能和评估标准生成器技能。求解器通过标准级反馈更新；评估标准生成器通过两类互补审核更新：需求覆盖审计和响应区分审计，两者均独立于求解器的聚合得分，避免评估标准被求解器当前表现误导。

Result: 在五个基准测试和三个 LLM 骨干网络上，DecoEvo 在所有基准测试的官方评估中均优于所有对比方法，在五个基准测试的平均值上相比 SkillOpt 获得 2.8-5.0% 的相对提升。

Conclusion: 通过解耦求解器与评估标准的优化目标，DecoEvo 能持续暴露求解器弱点并减少对已满足标准的重复强调，从而在开放式任务中实现更有效的文本空间优化。

Abstract: Text-space optimization adapts large language models (LLMs) by editing external natural-language artifacts rather than model weights, so the optimized artifacts remain inspectable and the model can be treated as a black box. However, most existing text-space methods keep evaluation fixed. On open-ended tasks, this can become a bottleneck: once the solver improves on the criteria a rubric measures, omitted dimensions remain invisible to the optimization signal. Simply evolving the rubric is also unreliable when updates are selected by the current solver's score, because apparent progress can come from making the rubric easier to satisfy. We introduce DecoEvo (Decoupled Co-Evolution), which co-evolves a solver skill and a rubric-generator skill under decoupled objectives without using gold rubrics during optimization. The solver skill is updated using criterion-level feedback, while the rubric-generator skill is revised through complementary audits of requirement coverage and response discrimination that are independent of aggregate solver score. This separation focuses generator updates on newly exposed solver weaknesses, reducing repeated emphasis on criteria the solver already satisfies. Under each benchmark's official evaluation, DecoEvo outperforms all compared methods across five benchmarks and three LLM backbones, yielding 2.8--5.0\% relative gains over SkillOpt in the five-benchmark average.

</details>


### [96] [Cognivia: A Cognitive Behavioral Therapy Copilot for Evidence-Based Mental Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25681)
*Qi Chen,Siria Xiyueyao Luo,Jian Wang,Yuan Shi,Haocong Rao,Xuejiao Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Cognivia是一个基于证据的AI心理治疗系统，通过整合认知扭曲自动识别和理性回应生成技术，在权威CBT数据基础上微调轻量级LLM，并构建了首个分层质量评估框架，在认知扭曲识别和理性回应生成方面显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知扭曲会加剧负面情绪并导致心理健康障碍，CBT虽有效但受限于专业治疗师短缺；现有LLM方法存在领域特异性不足、回应过于奉承以及缺乏认知扭曲标注等问题。

Method: 基于权威CBT文本构建框架，结合心理健康问答数据增强，采用多阶段提示和结构化生成策略，在行为科学专家监督下微调轻量级LLM得到Cognivia；同时提出首个分层质量评估框架。

Result: 在词汇指标、LLM-based Judge（两种互补标准）以及10位行为科学专家的人工评估中，Cognivia在认知扭曲识别和理性回应生成任务上均持续优于基线方法。

Conclusion: Cognivia有效解决了现有LLM心理健康应用中的领域特异性不足、回应过度奉承和标注缺失问题，展示了基于证据的AI心理治疗系统的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Cognitive distortion amplifies negative emotions and contributes to mental health disorders. Cognitive Behavioral Therapy (CBT) is an effective way to address cognitive distortions, but its large-scale application is limited by the shortage of professional therapists. Although large language models (LLMs) have recently been explored for mental health applications, existing methods still suffer from limited domain specificity, overly flattering responses, and the absence of well-defined annotations for cognitive distortions. This paper proposes Cognivia, an evidence-based artificial intelligence therapist that integrates automatic cognitive distortion identification and rational response generation. Our framework is built on authoritative CBT texts widely regarded as core paradigms and standard references. It is further augmented with mental health question-answer (Q and A) data, and employs multi-stage prompting and structured generation strategies under the supervision of behavioral science experts. Then we fine-tune a lightweight LLM on this augmented CBT dataset to obtain Cognivia. In addition, we propose the first hierarchical quality evaluation framework for assessing LLM-generated rational responses, developed through collaboration between AI researchers and behavioral science experts. Cognivia is evaluated using lexical metrics, LLM-based Judges with two complementary criteria, and human evaluation by 10 behavioral science experts. It consistently outperforms the baseline methods in cognitive distortion recognition and rational response generation, demonstrating its effectiveness. Our code is available at https://github.com/SNOWTEAM2023/Cognivia.

</details>


### [97] [Nudging Sustainable Choices through LLM-Generated Recommendation Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25726)
*Haya Halimeh,Dietmar Jannach,Oliver Müller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了推荐系统中基于生成式AI的可持续性解释如何作为行为助推影响用户选择。通过两项随机实验（N=529）发现，单纯披露可持续性信息不会改变用户选择，而框架性信息或描述性社会规范显著增加了可持续选择并简化了决策过程，但感知与行为存在分离现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统作为日常消费的中介，为促进可持续选择提供了潜在渠道。先前研究表明解释能影响用户对推荐的感知并支持更明智的决策，但解释是否可以通过突出可持续性信息作为行为助推尚不清楚。作者旨在探究不同行为框架下的可持续性信息在推荐解释中如何影响用户选择与感知。

Method: 使用生成式AI（LLM）基于助推理论生成可持续性感知的解释，通过人工评估和LLM-as-a-judge审核验证。随后在两个领域（低卷入度：速溶咖啡；高卷入度：酒店预订）进行两项随机实验（N=529），参与者从偏好匹配的推荐中结合不同解释进行选择。

Result: 两个领域均显示：仅披露可持续性信息不改变选择行为；框架性信息或描述性社会规范显著增加可持续选择并简化决策；感知与行为分离——单纯披露能提升解释评价但未转化为可持续选择行为。

Conclusion: 证明了LLM可以大规模生成基于理论的解释，指向了基于解释的社会善行干预实践，并讨论了生成式AI下自适应解释设计的启示。

Abstract: Recommender systems mediate everyday consumption, offering a promising channel for encouraging sustainable choices. Prior research shows that explanations influence users' perceptions of recommendations and can support more informed decisions. We argue that explanations can also serve as behavioral nudges by foregrounding sustainability information at the moment of choice. This study investigates how different behavioral framings of sustainability information in recommendation explanations affect user choices and perceptions. Using generative AI, we generate sustainability-aware explanations by drawing on nudge theory and validate them through human evaluation and LLM-as-a-judge audits. Building on this foundation, we conduct two randomized studies ($N = 529$) in a low involvement domain (instant coffee) and a high involvement domain (hotel bookings), in which participants choose among preference matched recommendations accompanied by these explanations. Our results show that, across both domains, merely disclosing sustainability information in explanations does not change choices, whereas framing that information or invoking a descriptive social norm significantly increases sustainable selections and eases decision-making. Notably, perception and behavior diverge, as plain disclosure improves explanation evaluations without translating into more sustainable selection behavior. Our work demonstrates how LLMs can generate theory-grounded explanations at scale, pointing toward practical explanation-based interventions for social good. We conclude by discussing implications for adaptive explanation design with generative AI.

</details>


### [98] [Loss Invariance Determines What Concept Layers Encode: Volume Grounding in Echocardiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25748)
*Hyunkyung Han,Min Jung Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现概念瓶颈模型在左心室射血分数估计中，即使概念（心室容积）预测准确，也可能因训练目标的尺度不变性而丧失物理意义，建议验证中间变量时需考虑目标函数的不变性结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念瓶颈模型通常以概念预测准确度作为有效性评判标准，但这一标准是否充分尚不清楚。本文通过超声心动图视频中的射血分数估计任务，探究仅靠概念准确度判断模型可靠性的局限性。

Method: 使用视频Transformer编码器在公开超声心动图数据集上训练，以左心室收缩末期和舒张末期容积作为概念层，射血分数通过容积解析计算（无残差连接）。对比仅用射血分数目标训练与额外用容积（毫升）监督训练两种方式，在1276个留出样本上评估。

Result: 概念瓶颈模型与直接回归的射血分数误差相当（6.89 vs 7.13 MAE）。但无容积监督时，预测容积的分布坍缩至0.1毫升（参考分布为35.7和45.7毫升），相关性部分保留；这源于射血分数作为比值的尺度不变性。加入绝对单位监督后容积误差从89.8降至25.8毫升，射血分数误差仅增加0.4。

Conclusion: 概念准确度本身可能掩盖概念层缺乏物理尺度的问题。临床模型中的可解释中间变量应针对训练目标的不变性结构进行验证，而不仅限于预测准确度。

Abstract: Objective: Concept bottleneck models route prediction through interpretable intermediate variables, and their validity is normally judged by how accurately those variables are predicted. We ask whether that judgement is sufficient, using left ventricular volumes as the concepts underlying ejection fraction estimation from echocardiographic video.
  Methods: A video transformer encoder was trained on a publicly available echocardiography dataset. End-systolic and end-diastolic volumes formed a concept layer from which ejection fraction was computed analytically, with no residual path to the output. We compared training under an ejection fraction objective alone against training with additional supervision of the volumes in millilitres, and evaluated both on 1276 held-out studies.
  Results: The concept bottleneck did not increase ejection fraction error relative to direct regression, at 6.89 against 7.13 mean absolute error. Without volume supervision, however, the spread of predicted volumes collapsed to 0.1 millilitres against reference spreads of 35.7 and 45.7 millilitres, while correlation was partly preserved. We show that this follows from an invariance property of the objective: ejection fraction is a ratio and is unchanged when both volumes are rescaled, so the loss determines the concept layer only up to scale. Supervision in absolute units reduced volume error from 89.8 to 25.8 millilitres at a cost of 0.4 in ejection fraction error.
  Conclusion: Concept accuracy alone can conceal a concept layer that carries no physical scale.
  Significance: Interpretable intermediate variables in clinical models should be validated against the invariance structure of the training objective, not only against prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [99] [Speculate While You Reason: Teaching Agents to Predict Their Next Tool Call via Joint Agent-Speculator RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25816)
*Jiabao Ji,Yujian Liu,Li An,Rohit Jain,Gungor Polatkan,Siyu Zhu,Shiyu Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出自推测智能体（Self-Speculating Agent），将智能体与推测器统一到同一模型中，通过联合强化学习方法，在不降低任务性能的前提下显著提升工具调用预测准确率（Hit@1提升17-18个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体在等待工具调用结果时存在大量延迟，现有工具调用推测方法（如独立的草稿模型或缓存轨迹）与部署智能体的实际行为存在对齐差距（speculator-agent gap）。

Method: 提出自推测智能体（Self-Speculating Agent），同一模型兼具智能体模式（解决问题）和推测器模式（从部分轨迹预测下一个工具调用），完全复用前缀KV缓存；提出联合智能体-推测器强化学习方法，从智能体自身 rollout 中推导推测目标，交替更新智能体和推测器。

Result: 在智能体搜索问答和对话式工具使用任务上，Qwen3-4B 的下一工具调用 Hit@1 从 44.1 提升至 61.2，Qwen3.5-4B 从 48.9 提升至 66.3，同时保持智能体任务成功率不变。

Conclusion: 将推测器与智能体统一到同一模型并通过联合强化学习训练，可以有效消除推测器-智能体对齐差距，在不牺牲任务性能的前提下大幅提升工具调用预测准确率，是一种更简洁高效的设计。

Abstract: Large language model agents often spend substantial wall-clock time waiting for tool call results. Tool-call speculation can hide this latency by predicting and pre-executing an agent's next tool call if the prediction matches the agent's eventual tool call, but existing speculators are typically separate draft models or cached traces that are poorly aligned with the deployed agent's own behavior. We identify this speculator-agent gap and show that the target agent itself is a strong next-call speculator. This points to a simpler design: unifying the agent and speculator within the same model. In this paper, we introduce the self-speculating agent, a single model that both solves tasks in agent mode and predicts its next tool call from partial trajectories in speculator mode, fully reusing prefix KV cache. To enable this dual-mode agent without degrading performance, we propose a joint agent-speculator reinforcement learning method, which derives speculation targets from the agent's own rollouts and alternates agent and speculator updates. Across agentic search QA and conversational tool-use agentic tasks, our method improves average next tool-call Hit@1 from 44.1 to 61.2 for Qwen3-4B and from 48.9 to 66.3 for Qwen3.5-4B, while preserving agent task success.

</details>


### [100] [Distributed Constraint Optimization via Online Learning and Iterative Pricing with Application to Large-Scale Satellite Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25835)
*Itai Zilberstein,Pranav Rajbhandari,Steve Chien,Tuomas Sandholm*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将在线学习算法用于分布式约束优化问题（DCOP）的均衡求解，并设计了一种迭代定价框架，将大规模DCOP分解为元级任务分配与局部调度两个子问题。两者结合在分布式卫星调度任务中实现了99%以上的观测请求满足率，远超基线方法的87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的DCOP实例规模过大，难以整体求解，需要高效的不完全算法和分解方法。

Method: 1) 重新审视DCOP与势博弈的联系，引入在线学习算法进行均衡求解；2) 提出一种新的分解框架，将DCOP分为高层元DCOP（任务分配）和低层独立局部优化（调度），并通过迭代定价方法更新元层效用以耦合两层。

Result: 在分布式卫星调度实例上，结合在线学习与迭代定价的方法实现了超过99%的观测请求满足率，而最先进基线方法仅为87%。

Conclusion: 所提出的在线学习算法在与代表性不完全DCOP算法的比较中具有竞争力，且与迭代定价框架结合后在大规模实际问题上取得了接近最优的性能。

Abstract: Distributed constraint optimization problems (DCOPs) provide a popular framework for distributed decision making under limited communication, but many real-world instances are too large to solve monolithically. We address this challenge from two complementary directions. We revisit the connection between DCOPs and potential games, and adapt modern online learning algorithms for equilibrium finding to DCOPs. We show that these algorithms are competitive with representative incomplete DCOP algorithms. We then turn to decomposition frameworks for large-scale DCOPs, motivated by large-scale decentralized satellite scheduling. We propose a new framework that separates a DCOP into two interacting subproblems: a high-level meta-DCOP for task allocation, and independent local optimization problems for scheduling. To couple the two levels, we develop a novel iterative pricing method that updates the meta-level utilities using feedback from the local optimizers. Combining our online learning methods with our iterative pricing framework, we obtain near-optimal performance on real-world decentralized satellite scheduling problem instances, fulfilling over 99% of observation requests compared with 87% for state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [101] [HiSkill: Empowering LLM Agents with Hierarchical Skill Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25853)
*Yu Hao,Jinxuan Cai,Qi Zhang,Yawen Li,Zhiqiang Zhang,Chuan Shi,Cheng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HiSkill提出了一种层次化技能图框架，通过构建包含技能节点、AtomicOp节点和多种类型边的有向图，连接高层技能与可执行动作模板，并在推理时通过子图引导LLM代理进行任务执行，在三个交互环境中取得了优于现有方法的效果并降低了推理token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹到技能方法通常产生扁平的、独立存储和检索的高层文本技能集合，未能充分利用技能之间的关系，且高层技能与可执行动作之间存在鸿沟。

Method: HiSkill构建层次化技能有向图，包含技能节点、AtomicOp节点及分解、时序转换、兼容、支持和恢复五种类型边；推理时检索任务相关子图，通过符号任务状态、当前活跃技能和子图引导LLM代理迭代地切换技能、选择AtomicOp并执行动作。

Result: 在三个交互环境中，HiSkill优于最先进基线方法，同时减少了推理时的token消耗。

Conclusion: 通过层次化技能图桥接高层技能与可执行动作接地是有效的，能够提升LLM代理在长时交互任务中的表现和效率。

Abstract: Skills have become an important abstraction for enabling large language model (LLM) agents to reuse past experience in long-horizon interactive tasks. However, existing trajectory-to-skill methods often produce flat collections of high-level textual skills that are stored and retrieved independently, leaving skill relations underutilized and maintaining a gap between high-level skills and executable actions. In this paper, we propose HiSkill, a hierarchical skill graph framework that organizes interaction trajectories into a directed graph with skill nodes, AtomicOp nodes, and typed edges. Specifically, the graph connects reusable high-level skills with executable action templates, while also capturing decomposition, temporal transition, compatibility, support, and recovery relations among them. At inference time, HiSkill retrieves a compact task-relevant subgraph and performs subgraph-guided task execution, where a symbolic task state, an active skill, and the retrieved subgraph guide the LLM agent to switch skills, select AtomicOps, and ground executable actions iteratively. Experiments on three interactive environments show that HiSkill outperforms state-of-the-art baselines while reducing inference token consumption, demonstrating the effectiveness of bridging high-level skills and executable action grounding through a hierarchical skill graph. Our data and code is available at https://github.com/BUPT-GAMMA/HiSkill.

</details>


### [102] [Runtime Uncertainty Monitoring for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems Using Bayesian Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25877)
*Bart Custers,Koorosh Aslansefat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于大语言模型的多智能体系统框架，利用令牌级对数概率和贝叶斯网络进行不确定性量化与传播，以支持精算风险建模。实验表明该框架在保持基准性能的同时，能提供工作流稳定性和运行时不确定性传播的额外洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精算风险建模属于高风险决策场景，LLM的概率特性和智能体间的依赖关系可能引入不可靠输出，导致风险评估错误、定价不公平及监管不合规。现有缺乏针对LLM多智能体系统中不确定性传播的系统性方法。

Method: 提出中心枢纽式多智能体框架，包含数据准备、建模、审核和解释等专业化智能体。核心创新是将令牌级对数概率经长度归一化后转换为校准的任务级置信度估计，再输入贝叶斯网络进行不确定性传播。

Result: 该框架能够复现基准精算模型的性能水平，同时额外提供工作流运行时的不确定性传播信息和系统稳定性洞察。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的多智能体系统结合不确定性量化方法，可以安全地应用于精算等高风险管理领域，为评估和信任此类AI系统提供新的技术路径。

Abstract: This paper investigates how multi-agent systems (MAS)-based on large language models (LLMs) can support actuarial risk modelling, with a particular focus on uncertainty quantification. Actuarial workflows represent a high-stakes decision-support setting where unreliable outputs may lead to incorrect risk assessment, unfair pricing, and regulatory non-compliance. To address uncertainty introduced by the probabilistic nature of LLMs and dependencies between agents, a multi-agent framework is proposed in which specialised agents perform data preparation, modelling, review, and explanation tasks under a central hub. The main contribution is a novel approach to uncertainty propagation using token-level log-probabilities and a Bayesian Network. Importantly, log probabilities are not treated as direct probabilities of correctness or task success. Instead, length-normalised log-probability summaries are transformed into calibrated task-level confidence estimates before incorporation into the Bayesian Network. Results show that the framework reproduces baseline actuarial performance while providing additional insight into workflow stability and runtime uncertainty propagation.

</details>


### [103] [Distributing Security Controls Through Harness Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25890)
*William Robert Gore*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何在商用AI编码代理上实现现成的安全控制，并通过自定义代理框架(SHarD)将其分发至分布式用户群体。SHarD基于Pi代理框架构建，集成了OS沙箱、技能扫描和工具限制三类安全控制，通过单命令安装即可达到与直接安装在商用代理上等效的安全效果，在23项OWASP测试中取得100%调整得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码代理的采用速度前所未有，但安全与风险问题仍然是跨组织扩展代理AI的主要障碍。现有安全控制未系统性分发给工程团队，而供应商原生方案会引入生态系统依赖，不适用于所有部署场景。

Method: 采用分阶段测试方法，在四种代理配置（两个商用代理有/无控制、基线框架、安全强化框架）上使用基于OWASP Top 10 for Agentic Applications的23项测试套件进行评估。SHarD基于Pi代理框架，集成了OS沙箱、技能扫描和工具限制三类安全控制。

Result: SHarD调整得分为100%，与最佳安全配置的商用代理持平，且在任何测试类别中均未出现回归。发现模型非确定性会导致不一致的安全结果，自主代理行为可能跨系统边界，而OS沙箱可直接缓解此问题。

Conclusion: 现成安全控制可通过自定义代理框架嵌入并分发给分布式用户，实现与商用代理直接部署等效的安全效果。提出了控制框架适应性框架的初始特征，并明确了第三个待研究问题。

Abstract: AI coding agents are being adopted at historic speed, yet security and risk concerns remain the primary barrier to scaling agentic AI across organizations. Existing security controls for coding agents are not systematically distributed to engineering teams, and vendor-native solutions introduce ecosystem dependencies that may not suit every deployment context. This paper investigates whether off-the-shelf security controls can be implemented on commercial AI coding agents and scaled to a distributed user base via a custom agent harness. A phased testing methodology was applied across four agent configurations --- two commercial agents with and without controls, a baseline harness, and a security-hardened harness --- using a 23-test suite derived from the OWASP Top 10 for Agentic Applications. SHarD (Secure Harness Distribution), a distributable harness built on the Pi agent harness, demonstrated that three categories of security controls --- OS sandboxing, skill scanning, and tool restriction --- can be embedded and distributed via a single install command while retaining equivalent efficacy to direct installation on commercial agents. SHarD achieved an adjusted score of 100\%, matching the best securely configured commercial agent, with no regression across any test category. Notable observations include evidence that model non-determinism produces inconsistent security outcomes and that autonomous agent behavior can cross system boundaries in ways that OS sandboxing directly mitigates. Initial characteristics toward a control harness fitness framework are proposed, and a third research question is identified for future investigation.

</details>


### [104] [Messier: A High-Resolution Corpus for Cross-Benchmark Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25891)
*Stefan Krsteski,Charlotte Meyer,Guillaume Allegre,Tony O'Halloran,Alexandre Sallinen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Messier整合了覆盖30个基准、714个智能体、11,891个任务和74,205个验证器的957,253条标准化记录，揭示了前沿进展在不同基准类型上的不均衡分布，并证明了严格的全通过聚合规则会掩盖进展并扭曲排名，同时建立了与Epoch评估能力指数高度相关的能力量表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体评估环境存在任务、脚手架、验证器和评分规则碎片化的问题，各研究聚焦于狭窄场景、规模有限或需要昂贵重运行，导致大部分实证记录无法相互比较。

Method: 构建统一语料库Messier，整合公开基准得分并在6个代表性不足的专业和科学领域补充五组智能体运行数据，每条记录按模型、脚手架、环境、任务、验证器和聚合规则标准化，并附带SOC/NAICS行业分类。通过反事实重新评分分析聚合规则的影响，并基于标准化记录推导能力量表。

Result: 前沿进展在不同基准类型上不均衡："函数调用"已饱和，"编程"进步最快，"企业工作流"最具挑战性；严格的全通过聚合规则会掩盖进展并人为改变智能体排名；推导的能力量表与Epoch评估能力指数排名的Spearman ρ=0.81。

Conclusion: Messier为智能体能力缩放、基准审计和评估失败细粒度分析提供了基础性、可复用的基础设施。

Abstract: Evaluating AI agents in interactive environments is hindered by fragmented tasks, scaffolds, verifiers, and scoring rules. Existing efforts focus on narrow settings, remain limited in scale, or require costly reruns, leaving much of the empirical record incomparable. We introduce Messier, a unified corpus of 957,253 records that span 30 benchmarks, 714 agents, 11,891 tasks, and 74,205 verifiers. Messier consolidates public benchmark scores and supplements them with five-agent runs across six underrepresented professional and scientific domains, including a recent legal benchmark. Each record is standardized by model, scaffold, environment, task, verifier, and aggregation rule, with SOC/NAICS classifications for occupational and industry analysis. Using this corpus, we show frontier progress is uneven across benchmark types, with "function calling" saturated, "programming" improving the fastest, and "enterprise workflows" remaining the most challenging. Furthermore, counterfactual rescoring shows that strict all-pass aggregation in multi-verifier tasks can obscure progress and artificially alter agent rankings. From these standardized records, we derive capability scales that align with Epoch's Evaluation Capability Index rankings at Spearman \r{ho} = 0.81 and can be specialized by domain, occupation, action space, or verifier type. Messier provides a foundational, reusable infrastructure for agent capability scaling, benchmark auditing, and fine-grained analysis of evaluation failures.

</details>


### [105] [Interactive Reward Agent: GUI Task Evaluation via Environment-State Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25904)
*Chenrui Shi,Yuwei Wu,Yang Liu,Ruining Feng,Zirui Shang,Zhi Gao,Lifeng Fan,Che Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于"提议-验证"框架的交互式奖励智能体(IRA)，通过交互式地获取和验证执行后环境中的证据来评估GUI任务。IRA在GUI-RewardBench上达到86.9%的准确率，并将GUI智能体的OSWorld成功率提升至34.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的GUI任务评估需要访问环境状态(如系统配置、文件数据、应用设置等)，仅依赖执行轨迹的截图难以获得全面信息，而现有方法在这方面表现不足。

Method: 提出基于"提议-验证"框架的交互式奖励智能体(IRA)：先根据任务指令提议任务完成条件，再通过调用系统工具、应用工具和GUI工具交互式地验证这些条件，综合可见界面和环境状态两方面的证据。

Result: 在包含321条GUI任务轨迹、覆盖10类Ubuntu桌面应用的GUI-RewardBench上达到86.9%准确率，优于现有评估方法；将IRA应用于GUI智能体的强化学习训练后，OSWorld成功率达到34.0%。

Conclusion: IRA能够为GUI智能体的训练提供有效的奖励信号，验证了交互式环境证据获取对于可靠GUI任务评估的重要性。

Abstract: Graphical user interface task evaluation aims to determine whether a GUI agent has successfully completed a user instruction. Automated GUI task evaluation has received increasing attention because the evaluation results can serve as reward signals for both test-time scaling and post-training. However, reliable GUI task evaluation remains challenging because the judgments often require access to environment states, such as system configurations, file data, and application settings, beyond the screenshots of execution trajectories. In this paper, we propose an interactive reward agent (IRA) based on a propose-then-verify framework to acquire and verify evidence from the post-execution environment. Given a task instruction and a GUI environment after the GUI agent execution, IRA first proposes the task completion conditions and then verifies them by invoking system tools, application tools, and GUI tools. This design combines evidence from both visible interfaces and the environment state in an interactive process. We further introduce GUI-RewardBench, a benchmark of 321 GUI task trajectories spanning 10 Ubuntu desktop application categories. Experiments show that IRA achieves 86.9% accuracy on GUI-RewardBench, outperforming existing evaluator baselines. We further apply IRA to reinforcement learning of GUI agents, achieving a 34.0% OSWorld success rate, which demonstrates that IRA can provide effective reward signals for training GUI agents.

</details>


### [106] [Penelope: Localized Latent Recurrence for Efficient Structured Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25915)
*Yutong Chen,Shouqian Shi,Xinran Liu,Haochen Wang,Jiaying Wang,Tianxing Xu,Yuanxi Wang,Zirui Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Penelope是一种针对预训练decoder-only Transformer的高效潜在推理框架，通过将循环计算局部化到选定的解码器区间，利用时间调制的GRU动态和循环读取状态进行潜在空间迭代优化，并采用渐进式CoT-to-latent课程，在减少推理延迟的同时保持有竞争力的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型在复杂结构化推理任务中增加计算主要通过扩大参数规模或序列化中间步骤（CoT），前者增加训练和部署成本，后者将推理计算与自回归输出长度绑定，存在效率瓶颈。作者旨在寻找一种更高效的方式，在不增加参数规模和避免长中间推理链的情况下分配额外计算。

Method: Penelope框架将循环计算局部化到选定的解码器区间：1）下解码器前缀一次性计算，构建问题条件的边界记忆；2）通过时间调制的GRU动态和循环读取状态对边界记忆进行迭代精炼；3）采用渐进式CoT-to-latent课程，将可见推理逐步转移到内部循环路径中，使额外计算在潜在空间分配，无需重复执行完整解码器或生成长中间轨迹。

Result: 在开源结构化推理基准上，在验证集选择的潜在预算下，Penelope相比已有的潜在推理模型取得了有竞争力的准确性，同时降低了实测推理延迟。

Conclusion: 潜在精炼可以局部化到狭窄的解码器区间，减少重复的全解码器执行，无需生成长的可视推理轨迹，为decoder-only Transformer模型提供了实用的精度-效率权衡。

Abstract: Complex structured reasoning tasks often require additional computation, yet current language models obtain it mainly by increasing parameter scale or by serializing intermediate steps as chain-of-thought (CoT) tokens. The former raises training and deployment costs, while the latter ties reasoning computation to autoregressive output length. We introduce Penelope, an efficient latent-reasoning framework for pretrained decoder-only Transformers that localizes recurrent computation to a selected decoder interval. The lower decoder prefix is evaluated once to construct a problem-conditioned boundary memory, which is then iteratively refined through time-modulated GRU dynamics and recurrent readout states before answer generation. A progressive CoT-to-latent curriculum transfers visible reasoning into this internal recurrent path, allowing additional computation to be allocated in latent space without repeatedly executing the complete decoder or generating a long intermediate trace. Experiments on open-source structured-reasoning benchmarks show that, at validation-selected latent budgets, Penelope attains competitive accuracy relative to established latent-reasoning models while reducing measured inference latency. These results show that latent refinement can be localized to a narrow decoder interval, reducing repeated full-decoder execution without generating a long visible reasoning trace and providing a practical accuracy-efficiency tradeoff for decoder-only Transformer models.

</details>


### [107] [dtControl2+$\varepsilon$: Trading Optimality for Explainability in MDPs via Decision Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25925)
*Tereza Kinská,Jan Křetínský,Tobias Meggendorfer,Sabine Rieder,Maximilian Weininger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对马尔可夫决策过程中的控制器解释问题，提出了允许一定精度损失(ε≥0)来构建更小、更易理解的决策树方法，扩展了dtControl2工具，在保证ε-最优性的前提下大幅减小决策树规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于决策树的控制器表示方法在大型系统或存在大量边界情况时，生成的决策树过于复杂，难以被人理解。直接减小决策树规模可能导致遗漏关键情况而产生错误控制器，需要一种在保证正确性前提下简化决策树的方法。

Method: 在马尔可夫决策过程(MDP)框架下，扩展dtControl2工具，引入ε功能：允许一定的不精确度ε≥0，通过"蒸馏"控制器核心本质，构建更小的决策树，同时保证ε-最优性（即损失在允许范围内）。

Result: 构建的决策树在规模上比现有最优方法小数个数量级。

Conclusion: 通过允许可控的微小精度损失，可以显著简化决策树表示，在控制器可解释性与最优性之间取得可调节的平衡，使大规模系统的控制器更易于人类理解。

Abstract: Over the past decade, decision trees have been used to represent controllers (a.k.a. policies) in an explainable way, with dtControl2 as a current state-of-the-art tool. However, for systems that are large or have many corner cases, even such representations tend to be too complex and not human-comprehensible. Unfortunately, reducing the size of the decision tree is not straightforward, as missing just a single crucial case might result in an incorrect controller. We tackle this issue in the setting of Markov decision processes, extending dtControl2 by "$\varepsilon$" functionality: Given an allowed imprecision $\varepsilon \geq 0$, we construct a smaller decision tree, distilling the essence of the controller, while still guaranteeing its $\varepsilon$-optimality. This enables us to provide tunably simpler explanations, omitting a controllable amount of detail. Our tool constructs decision trees that are orders of magnitude smaller than the state of the art.

</details>


### [108] [A Cost-Effective Multimodal LLM Reasoning Framework for Question Answering over Irregular Clinical Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25947)
*Frank Nie,Ethan B Liu,Yuan Zhu,Wei Fan,Jindong Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ClinPRISM提出了一种面向不规则临床时间序列（ICTS）的多模态大语言模型推理框架，通过不规则感知多尺度编码器、时间证据蒸馏器和渐进对齐策略，仅用16个时间序列令牌和0.15秒推理延迟，在4B参数LLM上实现了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型在处理临床时间序列问答时，无法有效应对临床数据中存在的稀疏性、异步性和不规则采样模式，亟需一种能够建模ICTS特性的高效推理框架。

Method: (1) 不规则感知多尺度编码器：在不同时间尺度上捕获稀疏临床证据；(2) 时间证据蒸馏器：跨尺度整合表示并压缩为少量LLM兼容令牌；(3) 渐进对齐策略：逐步将不规则轨迹与LLM文本嵌入空间对齐。此外构建了30K临床时间序列+多尺度描述对及41K指令微调实例。

Result: 基于4B参数LLM，在保留评估基准上达到SOTA，仅使用16个时间序列令牌，平均推理延迟为0.15秒/问题。

Conclusion: ClinPRISM以低成本实现了高效且准确的不规则临床时间序列问答，验证了多尺度编码与渐进对齐策略在处理ICTS数据中的有效性。

Abstract: Question answering (QA) over irregular clinical time series (ICTS) plays a pivotal role in a wide range of healthcare applications. Although recent multimodal time-series large language models (LLMs) have shown considerable promise in general-purpose time-series QA, they remain poorly equipped to model the sparsity, asynchrony, and irregular sampling patterns of clinical observations. To fill this gap, we propose ClinPRISM, a cost-effective multimodal LLM reasoning framework for question answering over ICTS data. First, we devise an irregularity-aware multi-scale encoder to capture sparse clinical evidence at diverse temporal scales. Then, we propose a temporal evidence distiller to integrate representations across these scales and compress them into a small number of LLM-compatible tokens. Moreover, we introduce a progressive alignment strategy that sequentially aligns the irregular trajectories with the LLM's textual embedding space. To facilitate training, we construct 30,000 clinical time series paired with multi-scale descriptions, together with 41,000 instruction-tuning instances spanning 11 tasks. Using a 4-billion-parameter LLM backbone, ClinPRISM achieves state-of-the-art performance on the held-out evaluation benchmark while using only 16 time-series tokens and achieving an average inference latency of 0.15 seconds per question.

</details>


### [109] [Large Language Model for Operations Research Formulation Selection in Multi-Warehouse Inventory Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25956)
*Jintao Xu,Yingzheng Ma,Jiong Dong,Yongzhi Qi,Jianshen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于求解器引导的大语言模型（LLM）框架，用于解决多仓库库存分配中因实例异构性导致的运筹学（OR）公式选择问题。该框架通过专家条件监督微调（SFT）进行模式学习，结合身份偏好优化（IPO）和群体相对策略优化（GRPO）进行偏好对齐。在京东真实数据集上，GRPO将命中率@1从21.45%提升至50.42%，分配准确率提升12.57个百分点，与事后最优解的差距缩小至4.85个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多仓库库存分配通常被建模为混合整数规划（MIP）问题，但没有任何单一公式能始终匹配由需求集中度、库存不平衡、补货规模、服务约束和预测波动性等因素导致的实例级异构模式。因此，需要针对每个实例动态选择最优的OR公式。

Method: 提出一种求解器引导的LLM框架：1) 构建专家条件监督微调（SFT）记录进行模式学习；2) 使用MIP求解器评估历史实例，将分配质量差距转化为边际加权身份偏好优化（IPO）偏好；3) 生成每个实例的专家评分元数据，在群体相对策略优化（GRPO）中作为奖励信号，对采样响应进行奖励分配。

Result: 在京东多仓库库存分配实例上，GRPO相比SFT+IPO选择器显著提升专家选择准确率：Hit Ratio@1从21.45%提升至50.42%，Hit Ratio@2从70.47%提升至82.31%。最终选择器分配准确率比现有基线提升12.57个百分点，优于SFT+IPO选择器和最佳固定OR专家，与事后最优解的差距缩小至4.85个百分点。

Conclusion: 求解器引导的LLM框架，特别是结合GRPO的训练策略，能够有效解决异构实例下的OR公式选择问题，显著提升多仓库库存分配的求解质量，在真实电商场景中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Multi-warehouse inventory allocation is typically formulated as a mixed-integer programming (MIP) problem, yet no single formulation consistently matches heterogeneous instance-level regimes induced by demand concentration, inventory imbalance, replenishment scale, service constraints, and forecast volatility. We study this issue as instance-wise operations research (OR) formulation selection, where each allocation instance is assigned to a solver-executable formulation from a candidate OR expert library. We propose a solver-guided large language model (LLM) framework for OR formulation selection, in which each OR expert corresponds to a MIP formulation encoding a distinct allocation priority. To train the selector, the framework first constructs balanced expert-conditioned supervised fine-tuning (SFT) records for schema learning, and then uses MIP solver evaluation on historical instances to convert solver-evaluated allocation-quality gaps into margin-weighted identity preference optimization (IPO) preferences and per-instance expert-score metadata for reward lookup during group relative policy optimization (GRPO) to assign rewards to sampled responses. Experiments on multi-warehouse inventory allocation instances from JD$\mathord{.}$com, one of China's largest e-retailers, demonstrate that GRPO substantially improves expert-selection accuracy relative to the SFT+IPO selector and, more importantly, produces higher realized allocation quality than both the preference-trained selector and the best fixed formulation. With GRPO, Hit Ratio@1 and Hit Ratio@2 increase from 21.45% to 50.42% and from 70.47% to 82.31%. The resulting selector achieves an allocation accuracy gain of 12.57 percentage points over the incumbent baseline, outperforming both the SFT+IPO selector and the best fixed OR expert, and reduces the gap to the ex-post oracle to 4.85 percentage points.

</details>


### [110] [CHARM: A Multimodal Graph Foundation Model with Hierarchical Context Modeling for Zero-Shot Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26023)
*Ankang Yang,Jitao Zhao,Di Jin,Yuxiao Huang,Dongxiao He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CHARM，一种多模态图基础模型，通过分层上下文建模实现零样本迁移，解决多模态图上零样本迁移中模态泛化与领域适配两大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界图数据常包含文本、图像等多模态信息，但现有图基础模型（GNN-based需下游适配，LLM-based主要处理单模态图）无法有效支持多模态图上的零样本迁移，而跨领域标注成本高昂。

Method: 提出CHARM模型：1) 用分层图上下文替换孤立原始节点，捕获多模态语义和跨模态关系；2) 将领域特定节点模式映射到共享高层概念；3) 设计模态感知图上下文编码器，融合多模态信息与图结构，并将表示转化为图Token供LLM处理。

Result: 实验表明，CHARM在零样本多模态图任务上取得一致性改进。

Conclusion: 分层上下文建模能有效解耦领域特定结构与模态特征，实现多模态图上的零样本知识迁移，为通用图基础模型提供新范式。

Abstract: Graph foundation models (GFMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for transferring knowledge across graph domains and tasks. Real-world graphs associate nodes with text, images, and other modalities, making multimodal graphs essential for representing complex entities and relations. Moreover, collecting labels and adapting models for every new graph domain is costly and often infeasible, motivating zero-shot transfer. Unfortunately, zero-shot transfer on multimodal graphs remains underexplored. Existing GNN-based graph foundation models typically require downstream adaptation, whereas LLM-based graph methods mainly address unimodal graphs or tasks within a single domain. This setting presents two key challenges. First, models must generalize knowledge from individual modalities while capturing transferable cross-modal relations. Second, without target-domain fine-tuning, node representations remain entangled with domain-specific structures and modality-specific characteristics, obscuring shared concepts in unseen domains. To address these challenges, we propose CHARM, a multimodal graph foundation model with hierarchical context modeling for zero-shot transfer. CHARM replaces isolated raw nodes with hierarchical graph contexts that capture multimodal semantics and cross-modal relations. These contexts map domain-specific node patterns to shared high-level concepts, reducing reliance on target-domain supervision or adaptation. A modality-aware graph context encoder integrates multimodal information with graph structure and converts the resulting representations into graph tokens for a large language model . Experiments show consistent improvements on zero-shot multimodal graph tasks.

</details>


### [111] [Desktop-Delta Bench: Do Computer-Use Models Understand Desktop GUI Transitions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26041)
*Abhishek Pillai,Samir Kumar Nayak,Yuan Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Desktop-Delta Bench (DDB)，一个包含2,013个人工验证实例的离线步骤级基准测试，用于评估桌面计算机使用代理(CUA)在异步GUI环境中对动作因果过渡的重建能力。实验发现8个模型系列在时序排序和动作识别上存在显著差距，最佳精确匹配率仅约65%，且动作类别推断比定位更难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CUA基准测试主要衡量最终任务成功或单帧定位，无法隔离评估模型是否能够重建动作产生的因果、任务相关的状态过渡——这对于拒绝过时观测、验证进度以及从失败中恢复至关重要。由于推理、远程输入、应用渲染和截图捕获是异步的，下一观测可能延迟、遮挡、瞬变或无关，易被误读为进度而带入后续规划。

Method: DDB包含2,013个人工验证实例，来自跨约15个应用和50个任务领域的新型多应用Linux轨迹。设计两个互补任务：(1) 463个3帧时序排序实例（含105个跨轨迹干扰项）；(2) 1,550个前后对比对，标注5种动作及其载荷。评估8个模型系列的32种排序和16种单动作设置。

Result: 时序排序任务未饱和：最佳非干扰项和干扰项精确匹配率分别为65.1%和65.7%。任务上下文使干扰项识别提升6.9个百分点，但非干扰项精确匹配下降2.2个百分点；错误分析显示模型存在系统性复制A-B-C顺序的倾向。单动作结果中，动作类别推断比定位更难：点击F1为0.96，拖拽仅0.76，但识别出的拖拽通常定位良好。

Conclusion: DDB通过填补GUI定位与最终任务成功之间的缺失诊断层，补充了端到端基准测试，能够针对性地改进桌面CUA的验证、可靠性和恢复能力。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs) increasingly act through desktop GUIs to complete long-horizon tasks. Current benchmarks primarily measure end-task success or single-frame grounding. Neither isolates whether a model can reconstruct the causal, task-relevant transition produced by an action- crucial for rejecting stale observations, verifying progress, and recovering from failure. This is difficult because inference, remote input, app rendering, and screenshot capture are asynchronous: the next observation may be delayed, occluded, transient, or unrelated, then misread as progress and carried into subsequent planning. We introduce Desktop-Delta Bench (DDB), an offline step-level benchmark with 2,013 human-verified instances from novel, multi-app Linux trajectories across ~15 applications and 50 task domains. DDB trajectories targets 3 failure dimensions- state verification, source tracking, and context-aware control- through 2 complementary tasks: 463 3-frame temporal-ordering instances, including 105 with a cross-trajectory decoy, and 1,550 before-after pairs labeled from 5 actions + its payload. We evaluate 8 closed and open-source model families across 32 ordering and 16 single-action settings, observing consistent gaps. Ordering remains unsaturated: best non-decoy and decoy exact-match rates are 65.1% and 65.7%. Task context improves decoy identification by 6.9 percentage points but reduces non-decoy exact match by 2.2 points; error analysis reveals systematic copying of the presented A-B-C order. Single-action results show that inferring the action family is harder than locating it: click F1 is 0.96 vs, 0.76 for drag, while recognized drags are generally localized well. DDB, thus, complements end-to-end benchmarks by filling the missing diagnostic layer between GUI grounding and final task success, enabling targeted improvements to desktop CUA verification, reliability, and recovery.

</details>


### [112] [Falling Behind Drives Unsafe Development in an Idealised AI Race Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26034)
*Elias Fernández Domingos,The Anh Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过行为实验和演化模型，发现AI竞赛中的不安全开发行为更多源于竞争动态（如对手行为、落后恐惧），而非个人风险偏好，建议政策应聚焦减少竞争压力、促进合作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技术竞赛中速度与安全性之间存在张力，AI领域尤为突出，竞争压力常被认为会激励风险更大的开发行为，现有研究对此缺乏实证行为分析。

Method: 采用框架化行为实验，让成对参与者在不确定时间范围内重复选择安全与不安全开发，不安全开发提供更快进度和更高即时收益但积累私人风险（风险上限分10%、60%、90%三组），结合演化模型分析策略动态。

Result: 预注册的风险水平比较和风险偏好假设未获支持；不安全行为更多受对手行为（对手不安全后自己也更可能不安全）、相对位置（领先减少不安全、落后增加不安全）及首轮选择预测效应影响，而非风险偏好。

Conclusion: 不安全开发源于早期行为惯性、对手行为及落后恐惧，而非单纯风险偏好，政策应重点减少竞争压力并促进AI开发中的合作。

Abstract: Technological races create tension between speed and safety: actors may gain by moving faster than competitors, even when risky development is harmful. This is prominent in debates about artificial intelligence (AI), where competitive pressure is often argued to incentivise riskier, less safety-conscious development. We study this using a framed behavioural experiment based on an idealised AI race, in which paired participants repeatedly chose between Safe and Unsafe development under an uncertain time horizon. Unsafe development gave faster progress and higher immediate payoffs but accumulated private risk up to a treatment-specific maximum of 10\%, 60\%, or 90\%; the race's competitive structure was held constant, and only this maximum risk varied. Neither the pre-registered comparison between risk levels nor the role of elicited risk preferences was supported by the data. Instead, exploratory analyses motivated by the task's repeated structure show that Unsafe behaviour is shaped less by risk preferences than by the evolving strategic state of the race: participants are more likely to choose Unsafe after their opponent does so, being ahead reduces Unsafe play while falling behind increases it, and first-round choices predict later behaviour. To interpret these effects we introduce a reduced evolutionary model with four strategies -- Always Safe, Always Unsafe, Conditionally Safe, and Conditionally Antisocial Safe -- which reproduces the treatment effect and shows how conditional Unsafe behaviour can be favoured by competitive race dynamics. Together, the experiment and model show that unsafe development can emerge from early behavioural momentum, opponent behaviour, and fear of falling behind, rather than from risk preferences alone, suggesting policy should focus on reducing competitive pressure and promoting cooperation in AI development rather than only individual risk.

</details>


<div id='cs.CV'></div>

# cs.CV [[Back]](#toc)

### [113] [Adversarial Deepfake Generation and an Investigation of Purification-Based Adversarial Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25842)
*Junghyun Kim,Seunghyun Kim,Jiyoung Woo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了"Go To Germany"团队在ImageCLEF 2026深度伪造检测与生成任务中的方法。生成任务采用FLUX.1-dev+PuLID进行身份保持的面部合成，结合针对12个检测器的多模型PGD对抗攻击（含DiffJPEG-in-loop、MI/DI/EoT、自适应加权、两阶段热启动），最终生成得分0.4170。检测任务采用SigLIP+DINOv2（AI生成图像）和GenD-DINOv3（面部操作）的最大概率集成，对基线深度伪造准确率99.4%，但真实图像虚警率高，最终检测得分0.6986。此外，自研了基于纯化的对抗检测方法，发现EFFORT检测器在中值-3纯化下的原始|Δlogit|信号能以AUROC 0.81-0.98区分对抗输入与干净输入，推翻了简单骨干保留假说，并揭示了Q70处的JPEG质量悬崖效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参与ImageCLEF 2026深度伪造检测与生成任务，探索有效的深度伪造生成和检测方法，并深入理解对抗性检测机制。

Method: 生成任务：FLUX.1-dev+PuLID进行身份保持面部合成，结合多模型PGD对抗攻击（DiffJPEG-in-loop、MI/DI/EoT、自适应权重、两阶段热启动）同时攻击12个检测器。检测任务：SigLIP+DINOv2（AI生成图像检测）与GenD-DINOv3（面部操作检测）的最大概率集成。自研探索：基于纯化的对抗检测，比较三个检测信号家族在6个共享CLIP ViT-L/14骨干的检测器上的表现。

Result: 生成任务：对组织者检测器逃逸率90%，对参与者检测器逃逸率57.6%，最终生成得分0.4170。检测任务：基线深度伪造准确率99.4%，但真实图像虚警率高，最终检测得分0.6986。自研探索：EFFORT检测器在中值-3纯化下的原始|Δlogit|信号AUROC为0.81-0.98（跨四种对抗源），发现JPEG质量Q70处信号崩溃的悬崖效应。

Conclusion: 所提出的深度伪造生成方法能有效逃逸检测，但检测方法在真实图像上的虚警问题仍需解决。自研的基于纯化的对抗检测方法推翻了简单的骨干保留假说，揭示了JPEG压缩质量对检测信号的显著影响。

Abstract: This paper describes the participation of team "Go To Germany" in the ImageCLEF 2026 Deepfake Detection and Generation Task. For the image generation task, we employ FLUX.1-dev with PuLID for identity-preserving face synthesis, combined with a multi-model PGD adversarial attack targeting 12 detectors simultaneously (DiffJPEG-in-loop, MI/DI/EoT, adaptive weighting, two-stage warm-start). Our approach achieved 90% evasion against organizer detectors and 57.6% against participant detectors, with a final generation score of 0.4170. For the image detection task, we combine two complementary detectors - SigLIP+DINOv2 for AI-generated images and GenD-DINOv3 for face manipulations - in a max-probability ensemble, achieving 99.4% accuracy on baseline deepfakes but suffering from high false-positive rates on real images, resulting in a final detection score of 0.6986. Beyond the official submission, we conducted a self-initiated investigation of purification-based adversarial detection, comparing three families of detection signals across six detectors that share a CLIP ViT-L/14 backbone. We find that raw $|Δ\text{logit}|$ under median-3 purification, applied through the EFFORT detector, separates adversarial inputs from clean inputs with AUROC 0.81-0.98 across four adversarial source types - a finding that refutes the simple backbone-preservation hypothesis and exposes a sharp JPEG-quality cliff at Q70 where the signal collapses.

</details>


### [114] [Unifying Active Learning and Semi-Supervised Learning for Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25014)
*Bahram Jafrasteh,Cheng Wan,Heejong Kim,Johannes C. Paetzold,Qingyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RegAL统一框架，通过拓扑感知的Pareto优化将主动学习(AL)和半监督学习(SSL)耦合，在极低标注率下同时指导样本选择与未标注数据利用，在BraTS 2021、dHCP和ProstateX数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AL和SSL在医学图像分割中通常独立设计和优化，导致目标不一致和冷启动阶段训练不稳定，缺乏统一框架来同时解决标注样本选择和未标注数据利用问题。

Method: 提出RegAL框架，通过共享的拓扑感知Pareto优化耦合AL和SSL。沿三个维度（体素级不确定性、特征多样性、拓扑一致性）评估图像：选择解剖信息丰富的边缘案例进行标注，同时识别几何稳定的图谱候选用于微分同胚配准引导的数据增强，训练自监督Mean Teacher分割网络。

Result: 在BraTS 2021、dHCP和ProstateX三个数据集上，RegAL在极少量标注体积下保持稳定，在Dice和边界距离指标（ASD、HD95）上一致优于最先进的AL、SSL和主动半监督基线。

Conclusion: RegAL通过统一AL和SSL的耦合框架，有效解决了医学图像分割中极端标注稀缺场景下的冷启动问题，为实际有限标注场景提供了稳定且高性能的解决方案。

Abstract: In practical settings, medical image segmentation models are often developed with limited annotated data rather than fully labeled datasets. Training frequently begins in ultra-low labeled regimes where only a small number of volumes are annotated. In such scenarios, practitioners must simultaneously decide which cases to annotate and how to best use the remaining unlabeled data. Although active learning (AL) and semi-supervised learning (SSL) both target annotation scarcity, they are typically designed and optimized independently, resulting in objective mismatch and unstable training during early-stage "cold start" conditions. We propose RegAL, a unified active semi-supervised framework governed by a shared topology-aware Pareto optimization that couples sample acquisition with unlabeled data utilization. RegAL evaluates images along three complementary axes, voxel-wise uncertainty, feature diversity, and a novel topological consistency metric, to select anatomically informative edge cases for annotation. On the other hand, the same criteria are used to identify geometrically stable atlas candidates for diffeomorphic registration-guided augmentation to train a self-supervised Mean Teacher segmentation network. Across BraTS 2021, dHCP, and ProstateX, RegAL remains stable with few labeled volumes and consistently outperforms state-of-the-art AL, SSL, and active semi-supervised baselines across Dice and boundary-distance (ASD, HD95) metrics under extreme annotation scarcity.

</details>


### [115] [Gradient-Based Latent Decomposition Reveals Mechanisms of Feature Degradation in Weakly Supervised Mammography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24835)
*Vinceline Bertrand,Ionut Cardei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 弱监督分层VAE模型中，粗粒度病变类型特征在重建中得以保留，而细粒度恶性特征退化。通过梯度正交潜变量分解，发现仅~4.4%的潜变量与监督梯度对齐，其余95.6%的正交残差承载细粒度病理预测，解释了重建与分类性能的不对称性（AUC差距0.314，p<0.001）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱监督分层模型存在一个持续性不对称现象：粗粒度病变类型特征在重建时得以保留，而细粒度恶性特征却退化，这一模式直接影响乳腺癌筛查管线的临床可靠性。作者旨在从机制上解释这一不对称性。

Method: 提出基于梯度的正交潜变量分解方法用于分层变分自编码器（H-VAE），将潜空间划分为任务对齐分量（z₁，受粗粒度监督梯度塑造）和正交残差（z_res，捕获剩余表征能力）。在CBIS-DDSM数据集的3,550个乳腺ROI上进行实验。

Result: 仅~4.4%的潜变量幅度与监督梯度对齐，~95.6%位于正交残差中，细粒度病理预测主要依赖后者。Stage-1 AUC 0.866，Stage 2 AUC 0.552，重建稳定性差距Δ_diag=5%（p=0.005），分类差距Δ_AUC=0.314（p<0.001）。潜变量消融实验证实两类任务的特征主要位于z_res。与MIL和MTL的比较验证了跨架构和模态的泛化性。

Conclusion: 在高维空间中，单个粗粒度监督信号仅孤立出一个稀疏的1D潜变量方向，迫使关键的细粒度特征落入脆弱的残差子空间，从而结构性地解释了重建对病理稳定性产生不成比例退化的原因。

Abstract: Weakly supervised hierarchical models exhibit a persistent asymmetry: coarse lesion-type features are preserved under reconstruction while fine-grained malignancy cues degrade---a pattern with direct consequences for the clinical reliability of breast cancer screening pipelines. We introduce gradient-based orthogonal latent decomposition for hierarchical Variational Autoencoders~(H-VAEs) to mechanistically explain this asymmetry. The latent space is partitioned into a task-aligned component~($z_1$), shaped by coarse supervisory gradients, and an orthogonal residual~($z_{\text{res}}$) capturing remaining representational capacity. On~3,550 mammographic Regions of Interest~(ROIs) from CBIS-DDSM, only~$\sim$4.4\% of latent magnitude aligns with supervisory gradients, leaving~$\sim$95.6\% in the orthogonal residual upon which fine-grained pathology prediction primarily depends. The model achieves Stage-1~AUC~0.866 and Stage 2~AUC~0.552, with a reconstruction stability gap of $Δ_{\text{diag}}=5\%$ ($p=0.005$) and a classification gap of $Δ_{\text{AUC}}=0.314$ ($p{<}0.001$). Latent ablation confirms that features for both tasks reside heavily in~$z_{\text{res}}$, structurally explaining why reconstruction degrades pathology stability disproportionately. Comparisons with Multi-Instance Learning~(MIL) and Multi-Task Learning~(MTL) confirm generalization across architectures and modalities. These findings reveal that in high-dimensional spaces, a single coarse supervisory signal isolates only a sparse 1D latent direction, forcing critical fine-grained features into the vulnerable residual subspace.

</details>


### [116] [Track-Leakage-Free Hold-Out Self-Validation for Photogrammetric Reconstruction: Protocol, Sensitivity, and Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24852)
*Behnam Asadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种“无跟踪泄露的留出协议”（track-leakage-free hold-out protocol），用于评估3D重建的自一致性。实验表明，该协议能有效衡量内部几何一致性，但无法替代绝对精度评估，也不能作为通用粗差检测门控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光度测量类3D重建的正确性通常缺乏外部真值验证，现有方法无法自评估重建可靠性。作者希望探索：重建能否在没有地面真值的情况下自评估其可靠性？

Method: 提出无跟踪泄露的留出协议：保留确定性子集的图像，每张图像仅对至少由两张保留图像观察到的3D点进行重定位（track-level屏障），确保视图不会被它参与构建的结构所测试，聚合为mAA置信度。

Result: (i)良好重建可达毫度级自一致性；(ii)阈值化自一致性饱和，与绝对精度无稳定相关（相关系数-0.57至+0.98，95%CI跨越零）；(iii)仅当模型碎片化时检测到失效，单一自一致但全局扭曲的模型无法检测（4次捕获中3次错误55-106m但置信度为1.00）；(iv)ETH3D和IMC 2025基准验证了相同结论。

Conclusion: 无跟踪泄露的留出协议衡量的仅是内部几何一致性而非绝对精度，既不能替代控制点评估，也不能作为通用粗差检测门控。

Abstract: Automated photogrammetric inspection emits metric measurements from a 3D reconstruction whose own correctness is normally unknown without an external survey. Can a reconstruction estimate its own reliability with no ground truth? We formalise a track-leakage-free hold-out protocol: a deterministic subset of images is withheld and each re-localised against only 3D points seen by at least two retained images -- a track-level barrier so a view is never tested against structure it helped create -- aggregated into an mAA confidence. Held-out observations still entered the bundle adjustment that built the trusted structure, so we call it track-leakage-free (a stricter re-mapping variant confirms this at 3/5 deg). Across operational GNSS-referenced captures plus ETH3D, EuRoC and the IMC 2025 benchmark we report four findings. (i) The protocol is computationally well-posed: good reconstructions score millidegree self-consistency. (ii) Thresholded self-consistency saturates and does not track absolute accuracy: confidence stays near 1.00 while true RTK error swings up to 14x within a capture, and the per-capture correlation is sign-unstable across five captures (-0.57 to +0.98; pooled 95% CI spanning zero), so it cannot gate accuracy. (iii) It flags gross failure only when the failure destroys internal consistency: a fragmenting model drops confidence, but a single self-consistent, globally-distorted model evades it -- three of four captures gave one model wrong by 55-106 m at confidence 1.00. (iv) The same dichotomy holds on independent ETH3D and IMC 2025 ground truth. Track-leakage-free hold-out measures internal geometric consistency, not absolute accuracy: neither a substitute for control-point assessment nor a general gross-failure gate. We release the protocol and degradation harness.

</details>


### [117] [DisasterTD: Disaster Toponym Disambiguation Using Multimodal LLMs and Cross-View Geolocalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24856)
*Wenping Yin,Ziqi Liu,Naixia Mou,Weijia Li,Danfeng Hong,Hao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DisasterTD 框架，融合多模态大语言模型（MLLM）语义推理与跨视角地理定位，解决社交媒体图像中灾害地名消歧问题。在 Hurricane Harvey 数据集上，该方法在模糊地名场景下表现显著优于纯 MLLM 或纯跨视角基线，50m 内精度达 47.01%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体图像虽能提供及时、细粒度的灾害地面信息，但其地理参考常模糊或歧义，导致精确定位困难，亟需有效的地名消歧方法。

Method: 提出 DisasterTD 框架：1）利用 MLLM 从嘈杂文本中提取地名并生成候选地理位置；2）通过社交媒体图像与遥感图像（及可选街景图像）的跨视角匹配，验证并精炼候选结果。

Result: 在 Hurricane Harvey 数据集上，DisasterTD 在 1000m/500m/250m/100m/50m 精度分别达 71.62%/62.36%/57.99%/52.09%/47.01%，平均误差 11.33km，中位误差 0.68km，在模糊地名场景下提升最显著。

Conclusion: 将 MLLM 候选生成与跨视角验证相结合，能有效实现细粒度灾害地理定位，尤其在模糊地名场景下表现突出。

Abstract: Social media imagery (SMI) provides timely and fine-grained ground perspectives that are valuable for situational awareness and emergency response. Unlike satellite or aerial imagery, SMI can capture disaster impacts and ground-level conditions in a timely manner. However, geographic references in SMI are often vague or ambiguous, making accurate geolocalization challenging. To address this issue, we propose DisasterTD, a disaster toponym disambiguation framework that integrates multimodal large language model (MLLMs)-based semantic reasoning with cross-view geolocalization. First, MLLMs extract toponyms and generate candidate geolocations from noisy textual inputs. Then, cross-view matching between SMI, remote sensing imagery (RSI), and optionally street-view imagery (SVI) is used to verify and refine these candidate results. We evaluate DisasterTD on the Hurricane Harvey dataset, where SMI is augmented with collected RSI and SVI to construct a cross-view benchmark for disaster geolocalization. The dataset is divided into four categories based on toponym clarity and ambiguity, allowing a fine-grained performance analysis across scenarios. Results show that DisasterTD consistently outperforms MLLM-only and cross-view-only baselines without disambiguation, achieving geolocalization accuracies of 71.62% within 1000 m, 62.36% within 500 m, 57.99% within 250 m, 52.09% within 100 m, and 47.01% within 50 m, while reducing the mean and median errors to 11.33 km and 0.68 km, respectively. The largest improvements appear in ambiguous toponyms, where semantic reasoning with cross-view evidence reduces candidate dispersion and errors. These findings demonstrate the effectiveness of integrating MLLM-based candidate generation with cross-view verification for fine-grained disaster geolocalization.

</details>


### [118] [Harm is not Universal: Community-Specific Toxicity Detection is Urgently Needed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24898)
*Xinnuo Xu,Anja Thieme,Daniela Massiceti,Ioana Tanase,Rita Marques,Melanie Fernandez Pradier,Martin Grayson,Camilla Longden,Cecily Morrison*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出当前文本到图像生成中的毒性检测器采用"一刀切"方法，无法有效保护边缘化社区（如矮小症和盲人/低视力群体）。作者提出社区特异性毒性检测（CTD），通过与残障专家合作开发指南，实验证明现有检测器在零样本设置下F1分数低于随机猜测（0.32和0.37），而提示自适应方法和参数高效微调虽有提升（最佳F1达0.78和0.59），但仍远低于通用检测的0.9，凸显挑战和持续研究的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I毒性检测器采用通用模型对所有用户应用固定安全准则，实证表明约35%被标记为安全的图像对残障社区而言是有害的，暴露了现有方法对边缘化群体的保护不足。

Method: 与残障专家合作，为矮小症和盲人/低视力两个社区开发安全准则；构建2400张T2I生成图像的标注数据集；评估多种方法：零样本设置下的大视觉语言模型和通用毒性检测器、基于提示的自适应方法（ICL、VQA）、参数高效微调（0.5B-7B模型，少于100个示例）。

Result: 零样本设置下F1分数仅为0.32和0.37，低于随机猜测；GPT-4o通过提示自适应达到F1 0.50和0.78；参数高效微调使小模型达到最佳F1 0.48和0.59；通用毒性检测F1约0.9，CTD性能仍远低于此水平。

Conclusion: 社区特异性毒性检测虽可行且提示自适应和微调方法有提升效果，但性能仍远低于通用检测，表明CTD是一个具有挑战性的问题，需要持续的研究投入。

Abstract: State-of-the-art toxicity detectors for text-to-image generation adopt a one-size-fits-all approach: a single universal model applying fixed safety guidelines to all users. Our empirical evidence shows that these detectors fail to shield marginalized communities: approximately 35% of generated images labeled safe are considered harmful by disability communities. In this position paper, we argue for community-specific toxicity detection (CTD). To demonstrate its feasibility, we collaborate with disability experts to develop safety guidelines for two communities: dwarfism and blind/low vision. Using a dataset of 2,400 annotated T2I-generated images we demonstrate that both large vision-language models and existing general-purpose toxicity detectors catastrophically fail to recognize harmful content under these guidelines in zero-shot settings with F1 score lower than random guessing (F1 0.32 and 0.37). Promisingly, prompt-based adaptation methods (ICL, VQA) substantially improve harm detection performance (GPT-4o: F1 0.50 and 0.78), while parameter-efficient fine-tuning improves smaller models (0.5b-7b with best F1 0.48 and 0.59) with less than 100 demonstrations, but remains sensitive to evolving guidelines. Despite these gains, CTD performance remains far below F1 $\approx 0.9$ achieved for general-purpose toxicity detection, highlighting the challenge and the need for sustained research effort.

</details>


### [119] [Mage-VL: An Efficient Codec-Native Streaming Multimodal Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24904)
*Senqiao Yang,Kaichen Zhang,Zhaoyang Jia,Jinghao Guo,Yifei Shen,Xinjie Zhang,Xiaoyi Zhang,Haoqing Wang,Xiao Li,Peng Zhang,Xiang An,Yin Xie,Zhening Liu,Xun Guo,Jiahao Li,Shicheng Zheng,Jinglu Wang,Zongyu Guo,Wenxuan Xie,Zihan Zheng,Yuxuan Luo,Bin Li,Yan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Mage-VL是一个面向实时多模态理解的编解码原生流式基础模型，通过Mage-ViT tokenizer选择性地编码动态熵丰富区域，减少75%以上视觉token消耗，4B模型在静态任务上媲美Qwen3-VL-4B，在视频理解、2D/3D空间推理上取得显著提升，推理速度提升3.5倍，并超越15B的Phi-4-reasoning-vision基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准视觉语言模型（VLM）遭受莫拉维克悖论：擅长复杂的离线视觉推理，但在简单的流式感知任务上表现不佳且处理效率低下。现有方法采用均匀帧采样，缺乏对动态、高熵区域的针对性编码，导致视觉token消耗过大，无法满足实时多模态理解与交互的需求。

Method: 提出Mage-VL，核心包括：(1) Mage-ViT自定义tokenizer，利用运动向量和残差能量在稀疏锚点帧(I帧)和预测帧(P帧)上选择性地编码动态熵丰富区域，在16x16 patch级别减少75%以上视觉token消耗；(2) 从零训练，使用约5.6亿未标记图像和1亿未标记视频帧；(3) 建立AI4AI数据管道，包含提示词-代码联合优化的多模态字幕生成和AI驱动的性能诊断；(4) 双系统架构：轻量级System 1事件门控（主动流式感知）和因果System 2解码器。

Result: Mage-VL-4B在静态任务上与Qwen3-VL-4B相匹配，在视频理解和2D/3D空间推理上取得显著提升，实现高达3.5倍的端到端推理加速，全面超越15B的Phi-4-reasoning-vision基线模型。提供七项关键实证发现，涵盖预训练数据效率、可变分辨率缩放、编解码系统加速、VideoQA SFT冗余、运动-空间协同、AI4AI数据管道和Zero-Vision SFT多模态强化学习。

Conclusion: Mage-VL通过编解码原生流式架构有效解决了VLM的莫拉维克悖论，在保持甚至提升静态任务性能的同时，大幅提高了流式感知任务的效率和视频理解能力。其创新的tokenizer设计、双系统架构和AI4AI数据管道为高效实时多模态基础模型提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Standard vision-language models (VLMs) suffer from Moravec's paradox: they excel at complex offline visual reasoning but struggle with simple streaming perception tasks and process them inefficiently. We present Mage-VL, an efficient codec-native streaming foundation model for real-time multimodal understanding and interaction. At its core, our custom tokenizer, Mage-ViT, replaces uniform frame sampling by selectively encoding dynamic, entropy-rich regions using motion vectors and residual energy across sparse anchor (I) and predicted (P) frames. Operating at a 16 x 16 patch level, this reduces visual token consumption by over 75% while preserving spatiotemporal context. Trained from scratch on approximately 560M unlabeled images and 100M unlabeled video frames, Mage-ViT matches or outperforms flagship encoders trained on billions of image-text pairs. We establish AI4AI data pipelines encompassing prompt-code joint optimization for multimodal captioning and AI-driven performance diagnosis to guide training recipes. Furthermore, through a bio-inspired dual-system architecture - a lightweight System 1 event gate and a causal System 2 decoder - Mage-VL enables proactive streaming perception. Extensive evaluations show that Mage-VL-4B matches Qwen3-VL-4B on static tasks while achieving strong gains in video understanding and 2D/3D spatial reasoning, with up to a 3.5x wall-clock inference speedup, and comprehensively surpasses the 15B Phi-4-reasoning-vision baseline. Beyond model artifacts, we deliver seven key empirical findings covering pre-training data efficiency, variable-resolution scaling, codec system acceleration, VideoQA SFT redundancy, motion-spatial synergy, AI4AI data pipelines, and Zero-Vision SFT for multimodal RL.

</details>


### [120] [PerceptionBench: Evaluating Atomic Visual Perception in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24957)
*Zichao Lin,Yifeng Xie,Bowen Qu,Haiming Wang,Jia Li,Haoning Wu,Yuhao Dong,Zuhao Yang,Jinguo Zhu,Haoyu Lu,Zijia Zhao,Tongtian Yue,Zhangyang Qi,Junwei Yang,Mengfan Dong,Peizhou Cao,Chenzhuang Du,Zaida Zhou,Haotian Yao,Hao Yang,Hongcheng Gao,Lin Sui,Weihong Li,Xinxing Zu,Jia Chen,Yao Wang,Xiaoxue Wu,Yalin Wang,Y. Charles,Yiping Bao,Yangyang Liu,Zhiqi Huang,Xinyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PerceptionBench是一个专门评估多模态大语言模型（MLLM）原子视觉感知能力的基准。通过分析42个现有基准中前沿MLLM的最早失败点，构建了包含十种原子感知能力的错误分类体系，并据此构建了3000个验证问题。实验表明，前沿MLLM在该基准上准确率均低于60%，感知相关幻觉是最薄弱能力，揭示了当前模型在原子视觉感知层面的系统性不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准无法有效隔离视觉感知能力：整体评估混淆了感知错误与推理或领域知识失败，而应用驱动基准仅覆盖狭窄、碎片化的领域。因此需要一种能够精准诊断原子级视觉感知能力的基准。

Method: 采用自下而上的方法：首先诊断42个现有基准中前沿MLLM反应的最早失败点，构建错误分类体系，其中感知分支定义了十种原子感知能力。据此构建3000个具有简短明确答案的验证问题，每个问题隔离单一能力，难度源于感知而非推理或知识。

Result: 16个前沿MLLM的测试结果显示：无模型准确率达60%，感知相关幻觉是平均最弱能力，相似整体得分掩盖了截然不同的能力轮廓。

Conclusion: 原子视觉感知能力仍远未解决，PerceptionBench为测量和诊断MLLM视觉感知边界提供了能力层面的标准。

Abstract: We introduce PerceptionBench, a benchmark specifically designed to evaluate the atomic visual perception capabilities of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). Existing benchmarks often fail to isolate perception: holistic evaluations conflate perceptual errors with failures in reasoning or domain knowledge, while application-driven benchmarks only cover narrow, fragmented domains shaped by heuristic designs. To address these limitations, PerceptionBench adopts a bottom-up approach: by diagnosing the earliest failure points in the responses of frontier MLLMs across 42 existing benchmarks, we construct an error taxonomy whose perception branch defines ten atomic perceptual capabilities. Guided by this taxonomy, we construct 3,000 verified questions with short, unambiguous answers, each isolating a single capability, with difficulty stemming from perception rather than reasoning or knowledge. Benchmark results across sixteen frontier MLLMs reveal that atomic perception remains largely unsolved---no model reaches 60\% accuracy, perception-related hallucination is the weakest capability on average, and similar overall scores conceal sharply divergent capability profiles. PerceptionBench thus provides a capability-level standard for measuring and diagnosing the visual perception boundaries of MLLMs.

</details>


### [121] [Enabling Fully Integer-Only Inference for Lightweight Detection Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24981)
*Thanh Cong Le,Michal Szczepanski,Martyna Poreba*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出I-LW-DETR，首个全整数运算的轻量级DETR检测器，通过Scale-preserving split convolution、SD-ShiftGELU和Constrained Shiftmax三个核心组件，在NPU和微控制器上实现端到端整数推理，仅适度精度损失，模型体积缩小约3.6倍，计算成本降低一个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Vision Transformer检测器在NPU和微控制器上难以部署，因为可变形注意力、特征融合和非线性激活函数等关键组件不原生支持整数运算。现有量化检测器要么保留Softmax、GELU、LayerNorm等算子，要么专注于重型骨干网络，缺乏轻量级检测Transformer的端到端整数实现。

Method: I-LW-DETR包含三个核心组件：1) Scale-preserving split convolution，为多尺度投影器的每个分支分配独立的激活缩放因子；2) SD-ShiftGELU，一种符号依赖的GELU近似，保留逐元素行为同时避免精度下降；3) Constrained Shiftmax，保持稳定的Softmax归一化。

Result: 在不同模型规模上，所提量化流水线均能生成高效的全整数模型，仅有适度精度损失，同时模型体积缩小约3.6倍，计算成本降低一个数量级以上。

Conclusion: I-LW-DETR是首个实现全整数前向推理的轻量级DETR，填补了轻量级检测Transformer端到端整数实现的空白，为在资源受限设备上部署视觉Transformer检测器提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Vision Transformer detectors now approach the accuracy of CNNs but remain difficult to deploy on NPUs and microcontrollers because key components, including deformable attention, feature fusion, and nonlinear activation functions, are not natively compatible with integer arithmetic. Existing quantized detectors either retain operators such as Softmax, GELU, and LayerNorm or focus on heavyweight backbones, leaving lightweight detection transformers without an end-to-end integer implementation. We address this gap with I-LW-DETR, the first fully integer-only lightweight DETR, in which every operation in the forward pass, including transformer nonlinearities, is executed in integer arithmetic. I-LW-DETR is built upon three key components: a scale-preserving split convolution that assigns independent activation scale to each branch of the multi-scale projector; SD-ShiftGELU, a sign-dependent GELU approximation that preserves element-wise behavior while avoiding the accuracy degradation; and a constrained Shiftmax that maintains stable Softmax normalization. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed quantization pipeline consistently produces efficient fully integer-only models across different model scales. Across all model scales, the proposed pipeline incurs only a moderate accuracy degradation while reducing the model size by approximately $3.6\times$ and the computational cost by more than one order of magnitude.

</details>


### [122] [Diff-ID: Identity Consistent Facial Image Generation and Morphing via Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25078)
*Taimoor Rizwan,Sara Atito,Muhammad Awais,Zhenhua Feng,Josef Kittler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Diff-ID是一个基于扩散模型的框架，通过双交叉注意力适配器（ArcFace+CLIP嵌入）、伪判别器损失（基于ArcFace余弦相似度与时步加权）以及21万张自建标注数据集，在保持高身份一致性的同时实现逼真的面部图像生成。实验表明其在身份-真实感权衡上优于现有方法，并提出了联合评估身份保持与真实感的FIQ指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率面部图像合成中身份信息保持是核心挑战，现有方法在身份一致性与逼真度之间难以取得良好平衡，尤其对安全系统、生物认证等敏感应用至关重要。

Method: 1) 构建21万张从CelebA-HQ、FFHQ、LAION-Face合成的数据集，经微调BLIP模型标注以增强身份感知训练；2) 在微调后的Stable Diffusion UNet中集成ArcFace和CLIP嵌入的双交叉注意力适配器；3) 提出基于ArcFace余弦相似度与指数时步加权的伪判别器损失；4) 提出DDIM统一变形管线实现无需逐身份微调的面部插值；5) 提出联合评估指标FIQ（结合身份相似度与感知真实度）。

Result: Diff-ID在原始ArcFace人脸相似度上未超过InstantID，但取得了显著更低的FID和最强的基于FQ的身份-真实感权衡。

Conclusion: 身份保持与逼真度应联合评估而非孤立看待，高身份相似度并不保证输出真实感；FIQ作为互补指标可有效反映这一权衡关系。

Abstract: Generative diffusion models have revolutionized facial image synthesis, yet robust identity preservation in high resolution outputs remains a critical challenge. This issue is especially vital for security systems, biometric authentication, and privacy sensitive applications, where any drift in identity integrity can undermine trust and functionality. We introduce Diff-ID, a diffusion based framework that enforces identity consistency while delivering photorealistic quality. Central to our approach is a custom 210K image dataset synthesized from CelebA-HQ, FFHQ, and LAION-Face and captioned via a fine tuned BLIP model to bolster identity awareness during training. Diff-ID integrates ArcFace and CLIP embeddings through a dual cross attention adapter within a fine tuned Stable Diffusion UNet. To further reinforce identity fidelity, we propose a pseudo discriminator loss based on ArcFace cosine similarity with exponential timestep weighting. Experiments on held out and unseen faces show that Diff-ID does not exceed InstantID in raw ArcFace Face Similarity, but achieves substantially lower FID and the strongest FIQ based identity--realism trade off among the evaluated methods. We also present a unified DDIM based morphing pipeline that enables qualitative facial interpolation without per identity fine tuning. We further argue that identity preservation and photorealism should be evaluated jointly rather than in isolation, as high identity similarity alone does not guarantee realistic outputs. To make this trade off explicit, we report Face Image Quality (FIQ) as a complementary ratio based score that combines identity similarity and perceptual realism while keeping FS and FID as the primary metrics.

</details>


### [123] [MorphUNet: Alpha-Controlled Biometric Transport for Diffusion-Based Face Morphing Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25092)
*Taimoor Rizwan,Sara Atito,Zhenhua Feng,Muhammad Awais,Josef Kittler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MorphUNet，首个基于扩散模型的双亲面部变形攻击框架，通过可训练的双交叉注意力机制、CLIP外观特征和ArcFace身份特征的解耦与融合，在FEI和FRLL数据集上实现了最佳的变形攻击潜力（MAP分别为0.919和0.886）和FID（35.19和44.86），并在跨数据集检测中保持极高的攻击成功率（APCER达0.996和0.946）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面部变形攻击可创建能通过多个身份验证的合成图像，对边境控制和身份验证系统构成严重威胁，现有方法存在身份塌缩、图像质量不佳等问题。

Method: 提出MorphUNet扩散变形框架：1）将双亲生成建模为alpha控制的生物特征传输，分别用CLIP提取外观特征、ArcFace提取身份特征；2）在去噪U-Net中引入首个可训练的分离双交叉注意力机制（Biometric Transport Layer），每个父本有独立的token bank，通过alpha参数组合残差；3）使用DDIM反转潜在插值实现连贯去噪起始；4）采用弱父本引导选择策略，最大化较低父本相似度分数以减少塌缩。

Result: 在FEI和FRLL数据集上，MorphUNet在至少三个识别系统同时被欺骗时MAP达0.919和0.886；FID分别为35.19和44.86；同数据集设置下APCER@5%BPCER最高；跨数据集迁移检测中APCER达0.996（FEI）和0.946（FRLL）。

Conclusion: MorphUNet在变形攻击成功率、图像质量和检测规避能力上均优于StableMorph、MIPGAN-II和MorDIFF等基线方法，并通过CFD不可见身份压力测试验证了其在性别、族裔和相似度极端情况下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Face morphing attacks create synthetic images verifiable against multiple identities, threatening border control and identity verification systems. We introduce MorphUNet, a diffusion morphing framework formulating two-parent generation as alpha-controlled biometric transport: each parent is decomposed into CLIP appearance and ArcFace identity evidence, aligned into a CLIP-compatible token space, with the two contributors preserved as separate identity-aware token banks. To our knowledge, MorphUNet is the first diffusion-based morphing framework using trainable parent-separated dual cross-attention inside the denoising U-Net: a Biometric Transport Layer carrying parent-specific identity evidence through denoising, attending to each parent separately before combining residuals via the morphing parameter alpha. DDIM-inverted latent interpolation gives a coherent denoising start, while weaker-parent-guided selection favours morphs maximising the lower parent-similarity score, reducing collapse toward one contributor. We evaluate MorphUNet against three state-of-the-art baselines (StableMorph, MIPGAN-II, and MorDIFF) on FEI and FRLL using six recognition systems, and propose CFD-based unseen-identity stress testing across gender and ethnicity pairing, demographic shifts, and parent-similarity extremes. MorphUNet achieves the best Morphing Attack Potential (MAP) when at least three of six systems are fooled by one morph, reaching 0.919 on FEI and 0.886 on FRLL, and obtains the best FID on both datasets (35.19 FEI, 44.86 FRLL). It also gives the highest APCER at 5% BPCER in the same-dataset setting, and remains highly difficult to detect under cross-dataset transfer, with APCER 0.996 on FEI and 0.946 on FRLL. The full evaluation analyses MAP, MAD, per-system vulnerability, identity balance, image quality, top/bottom-similarity stress tests, and CFD unseen-identity robustness.

</details>


### [124] [IMPRINT: Image-Conditioned Query Enrichment for Long-Tail Object Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25106)
*Jelin Raphael Akkara,Filippo Ziliotto,Luciano Serafini,Lamberto Ballan,Tommaso Campari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: IMPRINT是一个零样本即插即用框架，通过从网络获取图像来丰富文本对象查询，提升查询式语义地图中的对象定位能力，从而改进长尾细粒度物体下的ObjectNav任务。实验表明图像条件查询在OVON和HSSD-rare基准上均提升了定位和导航性能，并揭示了检测质量是长尾具身导航的关键系统瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本ObjectNav方法依赖纯文本查询，在处理细粒度物体类别时语义不确定性增加、定位可靠性下降。作者希望通过引入图像信息来增强查询的语义丰富度，从而提升长尾物体场景下的导航性能。

Method: 提出IMPRINT框架：从网络检索与文本查询相关图像，用视觉语言模型编码后与查询式语义地图匹配生成相似度图，再聚合得到上下文感知的定位结果。该框架无需训练或修改底层导航策略。同时构建了HSSD-rare基准，包含语义上更具体的子类别来评估长尾行为。

Result: 在OVON和HSSD-rare两个基准上，图像条件查询一致地提升了物体定位准确率和端到端导航性能。进一步分析发现，定位增益转化为导航性能提升的关键依赖于下游检测质量，揭示了长尾具身导航中的系统瓶颈。

Conclusion: IMPRINT通过引入网络图像信息有效弥补了纯文本查询在细粒度物体导航中的不足，验证了多模态查询增强策略的有效性，同时指出了检测质量是当前长尾具身导航系统的关键瓶颈，为未来研究提供了方向。

Abstract: Embodied AI increasingly relies on queryable semantic maps built from pre-trained vision-language models to enable zero-shot Object Goal Navigation (ObjectNav). However, existing approaches typically depend on text-only queries, which become less reliable as semantic specificity increases toward fine-grained object categories. We introduce IMPRINT, a zero-shot plug-and-play framework that enriches textual object queries with web-sourced images to improve grounding in queryable maps. Retrieved images are encoded using a vision-language model, matched against the semantic map to produce similarity maps, and aggregated to yield context-aware localization. Notably, this requires no training or modification of the underlying navigation policy. To explicitly evaluate long-tail behavior, we present HSSD-rare, a new ObjectNav benchmark built on Habitat Synthetic Scenes and featuring semantically specific subcategories. Across both OVON and HSSD-rare, image-conditioned queries consistently improve object grounding and yield end-to-end navigation gains. Further analysis reveals that translating localization gains to navigation performance depends critically on downstream detection quality, highlighting a key systems bottleneck in long-tail embodied navigation.

</details>


### [125] [OPERA: Offline Policy-guided Expert Routing and Adaptation for Universal Biomedical Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25108)
*Zihan Li,Feiyang Liu,Dandan Shan,Ruibo Wang,Qingqi Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OPERA提出了一种基于离线策略的多专家集成框架，通过离线学习的路由策略和测试时自适应，无需重训练即可灵活组合不同专家模型应对生物医学图像分析中的分布偏移问题，在9个数据集上显著优于30多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学图像分析面临严重的分布偏移（不同扫描仪、协议、患者群体），导致高性能模型需要反复进行领域特定微调，该过程成本高昂，且在标签稀缺或隐私限制下不切实际。

Method: OPERA将专家权重分配视为离线策略学习问题：1）专家画像模块从少量验证集离线学习选择策略；2）通过温度调节进行置信度校准；3）基于无标签测试数据的批量级统计进行分布感知的自适应类权重调整；4）利用模型间一致性和预测熵进行实例级路由，将每个样本分配给最合适的专家。

Result: 在9个数据集（涵盖眼底照相、胸部X光、CT、MRI及多模态诊断基准）上与30多种基线方法在分类、分割和多模态任务上对比，OPERA一致提升了性能和校准质量。

Conclusion: 离线策略引导的专家智能体协调是实现无需重训练的可部署生物医学AI的实用路径。

Abstract: Biomedical image analysis spans diverse modalities and tasks, yet real-world deployment is hindered by severe distribution shifts across scanners, protocols, and patient populations. High-performing models consequently require repeated domain-specific fine-tuning, which is a costly cycle that becomes impractical when labels are scarce or privacy constraints limit data sharing. We propose OPERA (Offline Policy-guided Expert Routing and Adaptation), a multi-agent ensemble framework that addresses this deployment bottleneck by treating expert weight assignment as an offline policy learning problem: a routing policy is learned from a small validation set without gradient updates to any expert agent, then deployed with test-time adaptation to handle distribution shift. OPERA coordinates heterogeneous specialist agents through complementary mechanisms. The expert profiling module learns selection policies offline, enabling informed allocation of expertise. Each agent undergoes confidence calibration through temperature adjustment, ensuring more reliable probabilistic outputs. OPERA also incorporates distribution aware adaptation, where class weights are dynamically adjusted at the batch level using statistics derived from unlabeled test data.
