Instance level routing assigns each sample to the most suitable expert by leveraging inter model agreement and predictive entropy. We evaluate OPERA on 9 datasets covering fundus photography, chest X-ray, CT, MRI, and multimodal diagnostic benchmarks, comparing against 30+ baselines across classification, segmentation, and multimodal settings. OPERA consistently improves performance and calibration quality, demonstrating that offline policy-guided expert agents coordination is a practical path to deployable biomedical AI without retraining. Code is on \href{https://github.com/HUANGLIZI/OPERA}{GitHub}.

</details>


### [126] [LENS: Adaptive Spatio-Temporal Zooming for Keyframe Sampling in Long-Form Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25125)
*Ce Zhang,Jinxi He,Katia Sycara,Yaqi Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LENS是一种无需训练的关键帧采样框架，通过动态分配帧预算在空间放大（关注细节）和时间缩小（扩展上下文）之间平衡，从而提升多模态大模型在长视频理解上的表现，在Video-MME上准确率从53.3%提升至60.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在处理长视频时受限于上下文窗口，现有关键帧采样方法在空间细节与时间覆盖之间存在冲突，难以在搜索空间中有效权衡。

Method: 提出LENS框架，无需训练，根据文本查询动态决定何时进行空间放大（聚焦单帧内查询相关区域）和何时进行时间缩小（通过多帧聚合扩展时间范围），在有限的帧预算内自适应分配，实现多粒度推理。

Result: 在多个长视频基准上，LENS一致优于先前最先进的关键帧采样方法，相比均匀采样，在Video-MME上将Qwen2.5-VL的准确率从53.3%提升至60.7%。

Conclusion: LENS通过动态的空间-时间权衡策略，有效解决了长视频理解中细节与上下文的矛盾，显著提升了多模态大模型的性能，且无需额外训练。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress in Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs), understanding long-form videos is still bottlenecked by limited context windows. While recent keyframe sampling methods attempt to mitigate this by distilling video inputs into a compact set of query-relevant frames, navigating the vast spatio-temporal search space remains challenging, as spatial detail and temporal coverage often conflict. To address this, we introduce LENS, a training-free keyframe sampling framework that dynamically decides when to zoom in for fine-grained details and when to zoom out for broader context based on the text query. Concretely, LENS adaptively allocates a limited frame budget between spatial zoom-ins, which highlight query-relevant regions within individual frames, and temporal zoom-outs, which expand the temporal scope through multi-frame aggregation, enabling the model to reason across multiple granularities while capturing both high-fidelity details and long-range context. Across diverse long-form video benchmarks, LENS consistently outperforms prior state-of-the-art keyframe sampling methods and delivers substantial gains over uniform sampling, improving Video-MME accuracy from 53.3% to 60.7% with Qwen2.5-VL.Code is available at https://github.com/zhangce01/LENS.

</details>


### [127] [FIDAC: An Easy-to-use Pipeline to Extract and Interpret Interpersonal Distance From Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25146)
*Keshav Rastogi,Eugy Han,Jeremy N. Bailenson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为FIDAC的开源库，通过融合多个面部检测模型实现从视频中自动提取和分析人际距离信息，并包含了人工编码管道和深度畸变校正工具以提升准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人际距离蕴含着关于人们相互感知的重要信息，但从视频输入中难以有效提取和解读这些信息。

Method: 开发了FIDAC开源库，融合多个开源面部检测模型的数据以弥补单一模型的缺陷；引入更精确的追踪方法，包括人工编码管道和深度畸变校正的基准工具。

Result: 成功将面部检测结果转化为可操作的位置与人际距离数据，并通过多模型融合和校正工具提升了测量准确性。

Conclusion: 未来计划在不同深度和朝向条件下评估FIDAC测量人际距离的有效性，并进一步整合空间关系学分析功能如同步性。

Abstract: The distance between persons reveals significant information about their perception of each other. However, such information is not easily extractable and interpretable from video input. We developed an open-sourced library, Facial Interpersonal Distance Analysis and Coding (FIDAC) that transforms facial detection results into actionable data about location and interpersonal distance. This tool merges data from multiple open-source facial detection models, strategically compensating for gaps in any individual model. In addition, we include methods for more accurate tracking, such as a pipeline for human coding of the selection of faces and a benchmarking tool to reduce depth distortion. For next steps, we plan on building upon FIDAC by evaluating its effectiveness at measuring interpersonal distance at various depths and orientations while further integrating features of proxemic analysis such as synchrony into its software.

</details>


### [128] [OpenPVMapper: A Multi-source, Nationwide Database of Rooftop Photovoltaic Systems in France](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25153)
*Gabiel Kasmi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OpenPVMapper是一个覆盖法国本土的屋顶光伏安装多源数据库，通过整合深度学习航拍检测、OpenStreetMap和概率检测数据集，包含1,135,850个安装点（约15.01 GWp），精度约74-75%，以CC-BY许可证开源发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏开放、全面、安装级别的屋顶光伏系统数据集：公共注册表仅聚合超过容量阈值的数据，而遥感检测方法通常局限于单一方法、有限地理范围或单一时间点。

Method: 通过聚合并协调多个互补来源构建数据库：基于深度学习的全国航拍图像检测流程、OpenStreetMap数据以及概率性建筑级检测数据集。每个安装点记录来源、检测方法及人工验证标记。

Result: 包含1,135,850个安装点，总装机容量约15.01 GWp。对1,862个安装点的分层抽样人工审核显示整体精度约74-75%，跨独立源的相互验证带来了显著的量化精度提升。

Conclusion: 通过聚合独立源而非依赖单一检测方法，OpenPVMapper达到了任何单一源无法提供的置信水平，同时保持可扩展性以纳入更多数据源。数据库及完整源代码以CC-BY许可证开源发布。

Abstract: Rooftop photovoltaic (PV) systems account for the vast majority of PV grid connections, yet no open, comprehensive, installation-level dataset of these systems exists: public registries aggregate data only above a capacity threshold, and remote sensing-based detection efforts, while extensive, are typically confined to a single method, a limited geographic scope, or a single point in time. We introduce OpenPVMapper, a nationwide, multi-source database of rooftop PV installations in mainland France, built by aggregating and reconciling complementary sources: a deep learning-based detection pipeline deployed on nationwide aerial imagery, OpenStreetMap and a probabilistic building-level detection dataset. The resulting database contains 1,135,850 installations, totaling approximately 15.01~GWp of installed capacity, each documented with its provenance, detection method, and, where available, a manual validation flag. Manual review of a stratified sample of 1,862 installations places the database's overall precision at approximately 74--75\%, with corroboration across independent sources bringing a substantial, quantified precision gain. By aggregating independent sources rather than relying on any single detection method, OpenPVMapper reaches a level of confidence beyond what any one source could provide on its own, while remaining extensible to further sources as they become available. It is released under an open (CC-BY) license alongside the full source code used to build it.

</details>


### [129] [OrganLens: Organ-Specific Representation Learning for CT Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25164)
*Zhixuan Ge,Anqi Li,Sadeer Al-Kindi,Hanwen Xu,Wei Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OrganLens是一种通过自监督学习为CT影像生成器官特异性表征的方法，使用共享编码器并基于器官身份条件，无需外部分割掩码即可产生11种器官表征，在多项任务中提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CT基础模型多生成单一的整体卷表征，而解剖感知方法要么编码预分割的器官体积（丢失临床相关的周围上下文），要么将图像解耦为器官token组（在特征形成前未对共享编码器进行器官选择的条件化）。需要一种既能保持共享编码器又支持器官特异性表征学习的方法。

Method: OrganLens通过自监督学习实现器官特异性表征学习。核心包括：(1) 器官身份条件化共享CT编码器；(2) 器官特异性蒸馏和 anatomy-mask 监督塑造特征；(3) 解剖加权池化生成器官特异性表征。推理时共享模型无需外部分割掩码即可产生11种器官表征。

Result: 在CT-RATE、RAD-ChestCT、INSPECT和NLST数据集上评估：心脏表征将CT-RATE心脏肥大AUROC从0.910提升至0.953；肺部表征使NLST肺癌死亡率的Harrell C-index提升14.2%；全局表征在INSPECT上达到文本-图像Recall@10为33.09%，图像-文本为32.04%。

Conclusion: OrganLens提供了一种可扩展的器官特异性CT表征学习方法，使用共享编码器，在器官相关任务中解剖匹配的表征提供了更强的任务相关信号，同时全局表征保持广泛实用性。为医学研究社区提供了一个可复用的框架，用于跨队列和临床终点的器官特异性疾病研究。

Abstract: A CT examination captures multiple organs, but many biomedical questions concern abnormalities, prognosis, or longitudinal change in a specific organ. These questions require a separate representation for each organ within the same CT volume. Existing CT foundation models commonly produce a single volume-level representation, while recent anatomy-aware methods either encode pre-separated organ volumes or explicitly disentangle images into organ token groups. The former may remove clinically relevant surrounding context, while the latter does not condition a shared encoder on a selected organ before its features are formed. We introduce OrganLens for organ-specific representation learning through self-supervision. An organ identity conditions a shared CT encoder, while organ-specific distillation and anatomy-mask supervision shape features for anatomy-weighted pooling into organ-specific representations. At inference, the shared model produces 11 organ-specific representations without external segmentation masks. We evaluate OrganLens on CT-RATE, RAD-ChestCT, INSPECT, and NLST across diverse acquisitions and downstream evaluations. Relative to CT-pretrained DINOv2, heart representations raise CT-RATE cardiomegaly AUROC from 0.910 to 0.953, while lung representations improve the Harrell C-index for NLST lung-cancer mortality by 14.2\%. The global representation reaches INSPECT Recall@10 of 33.09\% and 32.04\% for text-to-image and image-to-text retrieval, respectively. Across organ-related tasks, anatomically matched representations provide stronger task-relevant signal, while the global representation retains broad utility. OrganLens offers a scalable approach to organ-specific CT representation learning with a shared encoder. More broadly, it provides the medical research community with a reusable framework for studying organ-specific disease across cohorts and clinical endpoints.

</details>


### [130] [LGFNet: A CTC-Guided Local-Global Fusion Framework for Single-Channel Sleep Staging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25197)
*Chongjian Wang,Zhenghang Hou,Junjie Gao,Xiaofang Zhong,Shiyuan Han,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LGFNet提出了一种基于CTC引导的序列到序列睡眠分期框架，通过局部-全局融合编码器、CTC-注意力联合训练和三阶段解码策略，在单通道低延迟场景下实现了跨数据集的鲁棒睡眠分期，显著优于现有方法，尤其在N1期和过渡阶段表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 睡眠分期面临长期时间依赖、模糊的阶段转换（尤其是N1期）、跨被试/采样率/导联的分布偏移等挑战，这些问题在可穿戴设备所需的单通道低延迟场景下进一步加剧。

Method: 1) 提出局部-全局融合编码器（Local-Global Fusion Encoder），联合建模细粒度时间动态和长程睡眠结构；2) 采用CTC-注意力联合训练范式，统一时间对齐与上下文依赖建模；3) 设计三阶段解码策略，利用CTC引导解码和Viterbi平滑减少误差累积并保证生理一致性。

Result: 在五个公开数据集上跨数据集评估，LGFNet一致优于最先进的单通道方法。在Sleep-EDF-78上，相比DMIN，准确率提升+1.27%，宏F1提升+1.74%，Kappa提升+1.93%，在N1期和过渡段上增益尤为显著。

Conclusion: LGFNet通过局部-全局融合编码、CTC-注意力联合训练和三阶段解码，有效解决了单通道低延迟睡眠分期的核心难题，展现出强鲁棒性和良好的跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Sleep staging remains challenging due to long-range temporal dependencies, ambiguous stage transitions-particularly in N1-and substantial distribution shifts across subjects, sampling rates, and EEG montages. These difficulties are further amplified in single-channel, low-latency scenarios required by wearable and real-world applications. To address these issues, we propose LGFNet, a CTC-guided sequence-to-sequence framework for robust sleep staging. LGFNet introduces a Local-Global Fusion encoder that jointly models fine-grained temporal dynamics and long-range sleep structure, overcoming the limitations of conventional serial hybrid architectures. A CTC-Attention joint training paradigm is adopted to unify temporal alignment with context-dependent modeling, enabling more accurate recognition of stage boundaries and transitions. Furthermore, a three-stage decoding strategy is devised, leveraging CTC-guided decoding and Viterbi-based smoothing to reduce error accumulation and enforce physiological consistency. Extensive cross-dataset evaluations on five public benchmarks demonstrate that LGFNet consistently outperforms state-of-the-art single-channel methods. In particular, on Sleep-EDF-78, LGFNet surpasses DMIN by +1.27% accuracy, +1.74% macro-F1, and +1.93% kappa, with pronounced gains on N1 and transition segments, highlighting its robustness and strong generalization across diverse sampling rates, montages, and recording environments.

</details>


### [131] [ObliCity: A Benchmark and Baseline for Roof-to-Ground Projection Displacement Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25210)
*Kai Li,Yupeng Deng,Ligao Deng,Zhihao Xi,Chenhao Wang,Jierui Zhang,Yingrui Ji,Yu Meng,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将倾斜遥感影像中建筑屋顶到足迹的偏移向量（RFOV）提取定义为独立学习任务，并构建了首个大规模基准数据集ObliCity，同时提出基于ODE的DragRoof框架，实现了对几何投影位移的显式校正，在性能上达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么忽略倾斜遥感影像中建筑屋顶与足迹间的几何投影位移，要么在分割框架中隐式处理，缺乏对几何校正的专门改进，导致空间结构严重失真。

Method: 1) 将RFOV提取定义为独立学习任务，将几何对齐与语义分割解耦；2) 构建ObliCity数据集，融合高分辨率无人机影像和全球卫星数据；3) 提出DragRoof框架，基于ODE模拟人类标注行为中“将屋顶拖向足迹”的连续过程，学习确定性、几何一致的偏移场，并通过结束令牌自适应确定收敛。

Result: 在ObliCity数据集上，DragRoof在RFOV提取任务上达到最优性能，推理步数更少，且在方向和长度精度上均表现更优。

Conclusion: 本文为倾斜遥感影像中投影位移校正建立了原理性基础，数据集和模型将为该领域后续研究提供支撑。

Abstract: Oblique-view urban remote sensing imagery inevitably exhibits geometric projection displacements between building roofs and footprints, leading to significant distortions in spatial structure. Existing approaches either ignore these deformations or handle them implicitly within segmentation-based frameworks, where progress is dominated by general segmentation advances rather than improvements in geometric correction. In this work, we explicitly define roof-to-footprint offset vector (RFOV) extraction as an independent learning task that decouples geometric alignment from semantic segmentation. To support this task, we introduce the Oblique City dataset (ObliCity), the first large-scale benchmark that integrates high-resolution UAV imagery and globally distributed satellite data, covering diverse city morphologies and camera perspectives. Methodologically, we reformulate DragOSM into DragRoof, an ODE-based framework inspired by human annotation behavior. By simulating the continuous process of dragging roofs toward their footprints, DragRoof learns deterministic, geometry-consistent offset fields and adaptively determines convergence through an end token. Extensive experiments on ObliCity demonstrate that DragRoof achieves state-of-the-art RFOV extraction performance, requiring fewer inference steps while delivering superior directional and length accuracy. Our dataset and model establish a principled foundation for studying projection displacement correction in oblique remote sensing imagery. The source code and dataset will be avaliable at https://github.com/likaiucas/DragRoof.

</details>


### [132] [WHTMix: Efficient Stereo Depth Estimation via Walsh-Hadamard Token Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25234)
*Prathyush Sajith,Emadeldeen Hamdan,Ahmet Enis Cetin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出用Walsh-Hadamard混合器替代立体视觉Transformer中的全局自注意力，将计算复杂度从二次降至对数线性，在合成驾驶数据上减少2.46倍模型计算量和2.65倍推理延迟，同时保持端点误差不变。此外还提出混合对数视差损失函数，在不增加计算开销的情况下减少远距离物体的误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 立体深度估计需要在高分辨率下以严格的延迟预算运行，但基于Transformer的匹配器中全局自注意力的计算量随像素数呈二次增长，成为运行时间的瓶颈。

Method: 1) 用数据无关的Walsh-Hadamard变换在变换域中以对数线性代价进行全局token混合，替代立体Transformer中计算复杂度为二次的联合自注意力阶段，同时保留数据相关的交叉注意力进行左右视图对应。2) 提出混合对数视差损失函数，对对应小视差（远距离）的像素赋予更高权重，减少远处物体的误差。

Result: 在合成驾驶数据上，该方法在端点误差与注意力基线持平的情况下，模型计算量降低2.46倍，单图像推理延迟降低2.65倍。复杂度分析表明收益由序列长度与通道宽度的比率决定，解释了高分辨率立体匹配为何特别受益而分类Transformer则不然，并在非立体长序列基准上验证了token-to-channel缩放规律。

Conclusion: 数据无关的Walsh-Hadamard混合器可以有效替代立体Transformer中的全局自注意力，大幅降低计算成本而不损失精度；混合对数视差损失函数则能在零额外计算开销下提升远距离物体的深度估计精度。

Abstract: Stereo depth estimation for driving, robotics and augmented reality must run at high resolution under tight latency budgets, yet in transformer-based matchers the global self-attention that aggregates scene context grows quadratically with the number of pixels and comes to dominate runtime. We show that the joint self-attention stage of a stereo transformer, whose role is to spread context across both views, can be replaced by a data-independent Walsh-Hadamard token mixer that mixes tokens globally in the transform domain at log-linear cost, while the data-dependent cross-attention that performs left-right correspondence is retained. On synthetic driving data the mixer matches the attention baseline in end-point error while reducing model compute by a factor of 2.46 and single-image inference latency by a factor of 2.65. A complexity analysis shows the benefit is governed by the ratio of sequence length to channel width, which explains why high-resolution stereo matching is a particularly favorable setting and why classification transformers are not; we confirm this token-to-channel scaling on non-stereo long-sequence benchmarks. Furthermore, we introduce a hybrid log-disparity loss function designed to up-weight small-disparity pixels corresponding to long-range objects. This approach reduces the error on distant objects without incurring any additional computational overhead.

</details>


### [133] [CD-RMOT-Bench: Benchmarking the Cross-Domain Referring Multi-Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25239)
*Xiangqun Zhang,Likai Wang,Zekun Qian,Ruize Han,Wei Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了跨域指代多目标跟踪（CD-RMOT）这一新问题，构建了统一的基准CD-RMOT-Bench（包含真实清晰域、数字孪生变体和真实恶劣域视频），并提出查询中心自适应（QCA）框架，实验表明域迁移严重降低RMOT性能，失败主因并非检测错误而是表达式条件化的时序关联与目标选择不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RMOT研究均在域内设置下进行，未探索语言引导跟踪在不可避免的视觉域迁移下的鲁棒性，因此需要对此新问题进行系统性研究。

Method: 构建CD-RMOT-Bench统一基准（含真实清晰域跟踪数据、对齐的数字孪生变体、真实恶劣域视频），支持受控天气/视角偏移分析和合成-真实迁移评估；提出查询中心自适应（QCA）框架以稳定桥接视觉轨迹与指代表达的查询空间。

Result: 域迁移严重降低RMOT性能，失败原因更关键的是不稳定的表达式条件化时序关联和目标选择，而非单纯的检测错误；QCA建立了强基线。

Conclusion: CD-RMOT是一个极具挑战的新问题，CD-RMOT-Bench为跨域语言引导跟踪开辟了新方向，QCA提供了有效基线。

Abstract: Referring multi-object tracking (RMOT) extends tracking from category-driven perception to language-guided understanding by grounding object trajectories in natural-language expressions. Despite recent progress, existing RMOT studies are largely conducted under in-domain settings, leaving the robustness of language-conditioned tracking under inevitable visual domain shifts unexplored. In this paper, we study Cross-Domain Referring Multi-Object Tracking (CD-RMOT), a new and challenging problem that evaluates whether an RMOT model trained on a labeled source domain can reliably follow natural-language expressions in an unlabeled target domain with different visual conditions. To support systematic study, we construct CD-RMOT-Bench, a unified benchmark that combines real clear-domain referring tracking data, aligned digital-twin variants, and real adverse-domain videos. CD-RMOT-Bench enables both controlled weather/viewpoint shift analysis and realistic synthetic-real transfer evaluation under a shared RMOT protocol. Further, we provide a Query-Centric Adaptation (QCA) framework, designed to stabilize the query space that bridges visual trajectories and referring expressions. Extensive experiments reveal that domain shifts severely degrade RMOT performance, where the failure is not merely caused by object detection errors but more critically by unstable expression-conditioned temporal association and target selection. QCA establishes a strong baseline, while CD-RMOT-Bench opens a new direction for robust language-guided tracking across visual domains.

</details>


### [134] [Medical world models in healthcare: foundations, applications, and challenges for trustworthy clinical translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25242)
*Zhaoyan Chen,Zhongxiu Cong,Zhuanfeng Jin,Wanshu Fan,Dongsheng Zhou,Qi Ai,Haifan Gong,Congyu Liao,Xiaofeng Liu,Cong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过系统综述和可复现的证据图谱，界定了医疗世界模型的概念边界、技术基础、应用领域和证据要求。该领域围绕四个核心能力展开：患者状态表征、时间动态建模、干预条件仿真和临床监督规划。尽管现有研究在轨迹预测和干预方案比较方面展示了技术可行性，但大多数仍为回顾性、任务特定或临床前研究，证据基础受到多项因素限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医学人工智能主要局限于静态预测，无法有效建模患者状态的动态演变过程及其对临床干预的响应。作者旨在系统梳理"医疗世界模型"这一新兴领域的概念框架和技术基础，评估现有证据质量，并为临床转化指明关键路径。

Method: 采用结构化叙述性综合与可复现证据图谱方法，筛选1,455条独特记录，最终纳入98篇文献，其中14项研究符合严格经验性定义。将领域组织为四个核心能力维度，覆盖影像、电子健康记录、治疗响应、生理信号、超声手术交互及人群健康模拟等多个应用域。

Result: 现有研究提供了轨迹预测和候选干预方案比较的技术可行性证据，但多数为回顾性、任务特定或临床前研究。证据基础受限于不完整的纵向干预数据、不一致的动作语义、有限的可因果识别性、长程误差累积、不充分的不确定性估计以及有限的外部验证。

Conclusion: 临床转化依赖于精确的干预表征、稳健的因果与机理基础、校准的轨迹级不确定性、安全约束的规划，以及面向临床有意义终点的前瞻性多中心验证。

Abstract: Medical world models offer a framework for extending medical artificial intelligence beyond static prediction by representing evolving patient states and modelling how they change over time and in response to clinical interventions. This Review defines the conceptual boundaries, technical foundations, application domains, and evidence requirements of the field through a structured narrative synthesis with reproducible evidence mapping.We screened 1,455 unique records and assembled a corpus of 98 sources, including 14 studies that met a strict empirical definition of a medical world model. The field is organised around four capabilities: patient state representation, temporal dynamics modelling, intervention-conditioned simulation, and clinician-supervised planning. Evidence spans medical imaging, longitudinal electronic health records, treatment response modelling, physiological and multimodal state modelling, ultrasound and surgical interaction, and population and health-system simulation; clinical digital twins are treated as a cross-cutting integration framework.Current studies provide early evidence of technical feasibility for trajectory forecasting and comparison of candidate interventions, but most remain retrospective, task-specific, or preclinical. The evidence base is further limited by incomplete longitudinal intervention data, inconsistent action semantics, limited causal identifiability, long-horizon error accumulation, inadequate uncertainty estimation, and limited external validation. Clinical translation will therefore depend on precise intervention representations, robust causal and mechanistic grounding, calibrated trajectory-level uncertainty, safety-constrained planning, and prospective multicentre validation against clinically meaningful endpoints.

</details>


### [135] [FORGE: Frame Orthogonality in Relevance Geometry for Long-Form Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25266)
*Ghazal Kaviani,Ghassan AlRegib*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FORGE是一种无需训练的模型无关方法，通过在预训练多模态嵌入空间中构建查询条件几何结构，统一了相关性和多样性，从而在推理时选择最大信息量的帧子集，显著提升了长视频理解中关键帧选择召回率和问答准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频序列中相关内容的密度随长度增加急剧下降，暴露更多不相关内容会降低MLLM的准确性。现有方法需要在推理时从大量帧中选择与查询最相关的子集，但缺乏有效平衡相关性与多样性的无训练方法。

Method: FORGE通过查询条件化嵌入空间几何结构，使覆盖独立查询相关方向的帧在空间中彼此远离，将相关性和多样性统一为单一目标，从而在预算内选择包含最多不同查询相关内容的帧子集。该方法无需训练，与模型无关。

Result: 在Video-MME和LongVideoBench上，16/32/64帧预算下，统一关键帧选择得分提升11.0-15.3分；Video-MME上K=64时关键帧召回率从0.204提升至0.415；在8种开源MLLM（4B-32B参数）上，问答准确率最高提升8.7分（vs均匀采样）和5.2分（vs最强基线）。

Conclusion: 将嵌入空间与查询的高维结构对齐是推理时视频理解的一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have enabled long-form video understanding at a scale that was not previously possible. However, the density of relevant content decreases sharply as video sequence length increases, and exposing the model to more irrelevant content measurably reduces its accuracy. In this paper, we address the problem of maximizing query-relevant information in a frame subset selected at inference time, without training. FORGE (Frame Orthogonality in Relevance Geometry) is a model-agnostic method that induces a query-conditioned geometry on a pretrained multimodal embedding space, unifying relevance and diversity into a single objective. In this space, frames that cover independent query-relevant directions are far apart, and selecting the subset of maximum information captures diverse query-relevant content within the budget. Experiments on Video-MME and LongVideoBench at budgets of 16, 32, and 64 frames show that FORGE improves the unified keyframe selection score by 11.0-15.3 points over the strongest training-free baseline and up to doubles keyframe recall (0.415 vs. 0.204 at K=64 on Video-MME). The gains extend to question answering, where accuracy improves in every evaluated setting across eight open-source MLLMs spanning 4B to 32B parameters, by up to 8.7 points over uniform sampling and 5.2 points over the strongest baseline. Our findings suggest that aligning the embedding space with the query's high-dimensional structure is a promising direction for inference-time video understanding.

</details>


### [136] [ScaleResfusion: Residual Rectified Flow based on Residual Vector Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25275)
*Zhenning Shi,Chen Xu,Junhao Zhang,Kefei Zhang,Linjie Liu,Zhedong Zheng,Tao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ScaleResfusion，一个基于预训练文本到图像整流流模型的可扩展扩散框架，用于真实世界图像恢复。核心是残差整流流，通过引入残差项R，从带噪低质量图像出发而非纯噪声，实现高效加速和参数高效微调，并引入知识蒸馏降低采样成本。在多个真实恢复任务上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散方法在真实图像恢复中存在两个关键挑战：从高斯噪声起步的方法速度慢且对退化输入保真度低；基于残差的方法通常从零训练，难以利用现代预训练生成先验。

Method: 提出残差整流流（Residual Rectified Flow），在标准整流流中引入残差项R，构建从带噪低质量图像到高质量图像的残差传输路径，并支持精确加速点。通过学习残差向量场，保持与预训练整流流模型的输出分布和线性扩散过程一致，实现参数高效微调。进一步采用知识蒸馏流水线降低采样成本。

Result: 在多个真实世界恢复任务上，ScaleResfusion以更高效率达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 该方法为利用大规模预训练扩散模型适配真实图像恢复提供了一种实用且可扩展的方案，代码和模型已开源。

Abstract: Real-world Image Restoration (Real-IR) aims to recover high-quality (HQ) images from complex and unknown degradations. Although recent diffusion-based methods have substantially improved perceptual quality, their current designs leave two key challenges unresolved. Methods that start from Gaussian noise are slow and often less faithful to the degraded input. Residual-based methods usually train from scratch, which makes it hard to exploit modern pre-trained generative priors. In this paper, we present ScaleResfusion, a scalable diffusion framework for real-world image restoration built on pre-trained text-to-image rectified-flow models. The core of our method is Residual Rectified Flow, which introduces the residual term R into Standard Rectified Flow. Instead of starting from pure noise, it uses a residual transport path that starts from noisy low-quality (LQ) images and admits an exact acceleration point. By learning the residual vector field, Residual Rectified Flow keeps the output distribution and linear diffusion process consistent with the pre-trained rectified-flow models. This makes parameter-efficient fine-tuning possible at scale. We further introduce a knowledge-distillation pipeline to reduce sampling cost while maintaining restoration quality. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world restoration tasks show that ScaleResfusion achieves state-of-the-art performance with much higher efficiency. These results suggest a practical and scalable way to adapt large pre-trained diffusion models to real-world image restoration. Our code and models are available at https://github.com/YukinoshitaLove/ScaleResfusion.

</details>


### [137] [FunnelAL: Retrieve-then-Rank Active Learning for Single-Class Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25276)
*Reihaneh Rostami,Brian Goodwin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FunnelAL是一个将推荐系统中的多阶段漏斗架构应用于单类别发现主动学习的新系统，通过检索-排序级联设计，在三个图像分类基准上实现了最佳F1分数和标注效率，并对标注噪声具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模监督学习面临两个挑战：在海量语料库中高效找到相关样本，以及在嵌入无法清晰分离类别时区分真阳性和视觉上易混淆的负样本。传统主动学习将样本选择视为单阶段过程，无法高效解决上述任一挑战。

Method: FunnelAL采用检索-排序的级联架构：(1)基于嵌入的检索评分将语料库缩小到可控候选集；(2)精度触发的排序阶段，先利用学习到的排序器(RankNet)在批次精度高时运作，收益递减后自动融合基于委员会的探索(QBC)；(3)标注者的标签反馈在后续迭代中优化两个阶段。

Result: 在三个图像分类基准上，完美标注器下FunnelAL取得最佳最终F1、最佳标注效率(AULC第一)和最少的标注轮次。在真实标注噪声率下，FunnelAL保持第一或统计持平，而传统不确定性方法性能下降2-3倍。

Conclusion: FunnelAL为多阶段推荐系统与主动学习之间提供了具体桥梁，首次将漏斗架构成功应用于数据标注场景。

Abstract: We present FunnelAL, a retrieve-then-rank active learning system for single-class discovery, which adapts the multi-stage funnel architecture of industrial recommender systems to data annotation. Large-scale supervised learning faces two challenges: efficiently finding relevant samples in a massive corpus, and distinguishing true positives from visually confusable negatives when embeddings do not cleanly separate classes. Conventional active learning offers a principled framework for reducing annotation cost, yet it treats sample selection as a single-stage process that addresses neither challenge efficiently. FunnelAL decomposes the problem into cascaded stages. Starting from a single positive and negative example, the system iterates through: (1) embedding-based retrieval scoring that narrows the corpus to a manageable candidate set; (2) a precision-triggered ranking stage that exploits a learned ranker (RankNet) while batch precision remains high, then automatically blends in committee-based exploration (QBC) once returns diminish; and (3) feedback from the annotator's labels that refines both stages in subsequent iterations. We evaluate on three diverse image classification benchmarks. With a perfect annotator, FunnelAL attains the best final F1 on all three benchmarks, the best annotation efficiency (first in AULC), and the fewest annotation rounds. The most recent single-class discovery methods (GAL, PF-MA) at best match its final quality, and only at consistently higher labeling cost. Under annotator labeling errors at realistic rates, FunnelAL remains first or statistically tied for first while classical uncertainty-based methods degrade two to three times faster. Our work provides a concrete bridge between multi-stage recommender systems and active learning.

</details>


### [138] [CLBench-V: Evaluating Multimodal Context Learning from Grounding to Knowledge Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25294)
*Lai Wei,Chengqi Li,Jiapeng Li,Ruina Hu,Yue Wang,Weiran Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLBench-V是一个多模态上下文学习基准测试，包含3,443个实例，覆盖科学、金融、长文档理解、空间推理和网络视觉问答等领域。当前最佳模型得分仅为0.2847，表明该任务尚未饱和。InternVL3.5-30B-A3B在上下文定位和新知识学习上最佳，Qwen3.5-Plus在新信息应用上最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文学习评估主要集中在文本上下文，而现实场景中的上下文往往是多模态的（如图表、报表、地图等）。缺乏一个系统性的多模态上下文学习基准来诊断模型在何处失败。

Method: 构建了CLBench-V基准，围绕三个维度组织任务：上下文定位、新信息应用和新知识学习。结合了转换后的公开基准和新构建数据集，并采用自动化构建和过滤流程来降低成本。测试了6个近期多模态模型共3,443个实例。

Result: 最佳模型总体得分仅0.2847。InternVL3.5-30B-A3B在上下文定位（0.2605）和新知识学习（0.3240）上最佳；Qwen3.5-Plus在新信息应用（0.2862）上最佳。还分析了法官可靠性、上下文长度、图片数量及典型失败案例。

Conclusion: 多模态上下文学习远未饱和，现有模型在不同维度上各有优劣，未来需要进一步提升模型在真实多模态场景中的上下文学习能力。

Abstract: Real-world tasks often require models to learn from task-specific context rather than relying only on pre-trained knowledge. While recent work has highlighted this capability as context learning, existing evaluations mainly focus on textual contexts. In many practical settings, however, the context to be learned from is multimodal: scientific findings are conveyed through figures and tables, financial indicators are scattered across converted reports, and spatial decisions depend on maps, scenes, or web pages. We introduce CLBench-V, a benchmark for multimodal context learning that addresses the difficulty of localizing where context use breaks down by organizing tasks around three dimensions: context grounding, new information application, and new knowledge learning. CLBench-V combines converted public benchmarks with newly constructed datasets spanning domains such as science, finance, long-document understanding, spatial reasoning, and web-based visual question answering. To reduce the cost of constructing domain-specific context-learning tasks, we further use automated construction and filtering procedures for our newly built datasets. Across 3,443 instances and six recent multimodal models, the best overall score is only 0.2847, indicating that multimodal context learning remains far from saturated. Moreover, InternVL3.5-30B-A3B performs best on context grounding and new knowledge learning, while Qwen3.5-Plus performs best on new information application. We further analyze judge reliability, context length, image count, and representative failure cases. Code is available at https://github.com/IamLihua/CLBench-V.

</details>


### [139] [MEDit-Bench: A Dataset for Evaluating Message-Driven Narrative Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25300)
*Katsuya Ogata,Zongshang Pang,Mayu Otani,Yuta Nakashima*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MEDit-Bench是一个用于评估信息驱动视频编辑的基准数据集，包含长视频与多种编辑消息及对应的专业编辑结果，揭示了不同消息导致显著不同的编辑结果。研究发现LLM作为裁判存在严重位置偏差，且消息的模糊性和上下文依赖性负相关于模型性能。实验表明最强模型在宽松阈值下接近人类时间对齐，但在严格标准下仍远不及人类，人类专业编辑始终更受青睐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频摘要基准将编辑意图简化为单一的消息无关的显著性概念，未能反映视频编辑本质上是信息驱动的多样性——同一源素材因叙事不同会产生不同的镜头选择。

Method: 构建MEDit-Bench数据集，为长视频配对多种编辑消息及对应专业编辑结果；提出基于时间对齐度量的自动评估协议；标注消息的模糊性和上下文依赖性分数；使用多模态大语言模型和强化微调基线进行实验。

Result: LLM-as-a-judge因严重位置偏差不可靠；消息的模糊性和上下文依赖性负相关于模型性能；强模型在宽松阈值下接近人类，但严格标准下所有模型落后于人类；人类感知研究确认专业编辑始终优于模型输出。

Conclusion: 当前视频编辑评估需要更细粒度的消息驱动视角，LLM裁判在此任务中不可靠，消息难度可作为有意义的分层因子，模型与人类在严格标准下仍存在巨大质量差距。

Abstract: Video editing is fundamentally message-driven: even from the same source footage, the selected shots change depending on the narrative the editor wishes to convey. Benchmarks for a closely related task, video summarization, reduce editorial intent to a single, message-agnostic notion of saliency and thus do not account for this diversity. For evaluating message-driven video editing, we present \textbf{MEDit-Bench}, a dataset and benchmark, which pairs long-form videos with multiple editing messages and multiple professionally produced edits per message, demonstrating that different messages yield substantially different edits from the same source. We define an automatic evaluation protocol based on temporal alignment metrics, and find that an LLM-as-a-judge preference, a natural proxy for narrative quality, is unreliable for this task due to severe position bias. We additionally annotate each message with ambiguity and contextfulness scores, and show that both dimensions negatively correlate with model performance, establishing message difficulty as a meaningful stratification factor. Experiments with state-of-the-art MLLMs and reinforcement fine-tuned baselines show that while strong models approach human temporal alignment at lenient thresholds, all models fall behind humans at stricter criteria. A human perceptual study further confirms a large quality gap, with professional human edits remaining consistently preferred over model outputs.

</details>


### [140] [Human-in-the-Loop Signature Bootstrapping for UAV Hyperspectral PFM-1 Mine Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25310)
*Sagar Lekhak,Prasanna Reddy Pulakurthi,Emmett J. Ientilucci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用无人机可见光-近红外高光谱成像结合光谱角映射器、匹配滤波器、自适应相干估计器和约束能量最小化四种方法检测PFM-1地雷，比较了三种光谱签名来源，并引入目标发现曲线和空间候选审查计数来评估实际筛查效率。结果表明，自适应相干估计器仅需两轮和九次候选审查即可确认所有目标区域，而光谱角映射器变体需要数千次审查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱成像适用于材料鉴别，但实际地雷筛查不仅取决于检测能力，还取决于在发现目标前需要检查多少虚假警报。现有研究缺乏对检测后审查效率的量化评估。

Method: 使用SAM、MF、ACE和CEM四种检测器，在无人机VNIR高光谱数据上进行PFM-1地雷检测。比较三种光谱签名：地面SVC测量光谱、完全信息的场景内核心像素签名、模拟人在回路中的签名引导。除ROC-AUC和平均精度外，引入目标发现曲线和空间候选审查计数作为评估指标。

Result: 完全审查引导可达到完全信息场景内签名的效果，但所需审查工作量差异巨大：ACE仅需两轮和九次候选审查即可确认全部七个目标区域；而SAM变体需要数千次候选审查才能定位最终目标。

Conclusion: 在高光谱地雷检测中，仅使用ROC曲线等传统指标不足以全面评估实际应用效果，目标发现曲线和审查计数能更好地反映操作效率。ACE在减少虚假警报审查方面具有显著优势。

Abstract: Hyperspectral imaging (HSI) is useful for material discrimination, but operational mine screening also depends on how many false alarms must be inspected before targets are found. This paper studies PFM-1 landmine detection in unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) visible and near-infrared (VNIR) HSI using spectral angle mapper (SAM), matched filter (MF), adaptive coherence estimator (ACE), and constrained energy minimization (CEM). We compare a ground-measured SVC signature, a fully informed in-scene core-pixel signature, and a simulated human-in-the-loop signature bootstrap. Besides receiver operating characteristic area under the curve and average precision, we report target-discovery curves and spatial candidate-review counts. Full-review bootstrapping reaches the fully informed in-scene signature case after all seven target regions are verified, but the required inspection effort varies strongly: ACE confirms all regions in two rounds and nine candidate inspections, whereas the SAM variants need thousands of candidate reviews for their final target locations. Code is available at https://github.com/SagarLekhak/IEEE_WHISPERS_2026_UAV_HSI_PFM1.

</details>


### [141] [Sense it with your eyes: Sensation Generation and Understanding for Advertisements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25314)
*Aysan Aghazadeh,Sina Malakouti,Adriana Kovashka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究感官广告的理解、评估与生成，提出了感官广告数据集、感官分类任务(SenseClass)、自动评估指标(SenseScore)以及多智能体生成框架(SAGA)，为感官驱动的视觉说服奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI在创意内容生成领域应用广泛，但广告如何通过视觉线索唤起感官体验这一重要问题尚未被系统探索。

Method: 构建了感官广告数据集，定义了感官分类任务(SenseClass)以评估LLM和MLLM；提出了自动评估指标SenseScore；并设计了多智能体框架SAGA用于感官广告生成。

Result: SenseScore与人工判断具有高度一致性；SAGA框架在信息-图像对齐、感官唤起和说服力方面均有提升。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了感官感知视觉说服的基础，为广告中感官线索的自动化分析与生成提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Sensory advertising evokes human senses through visual cues, enabling audiences to mentally simulate experiences and increasing persuasive impact. Despite the recent increase in using AI in generating and understanding creative and persuasive content, how advertisements visually evoke sensations remains largely unexplored. In this work, we introduce the first study of understanding, evaluating, and generating sensory ads. We introduce the Sensory Ad dataset, and define sensation classification tasks (SenseClass) to benchmark LLMs and MLLMs. We further propose SenseScore, an automated evaluation metric for sensation evocation achieving strong agreement with human judgments. Finally, we introduce the Sensory Ad Generation (SenseGen) task and propose SAGA, a multi-agent framework that improves message image alignment, sensory evocation, and persuasion. Our work establishes a foundation for sensory-aware visual persuasion.

</details>


### [142] [Beyond Background Bias: Saliency-Driven Prototype Alignment for Dataset Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25318)
*Yawen Zou,Wenqi Cai,Guang Li,Ling Xiao,Chunzhi Gu,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于显著性驱动的数据集蒸馏框架（Saliency-Driven Distillation），通过构建类别判别性潜在原型来增强扩散模型在数据集蒸馏中的结构连贯性和泛化能力，在两个阶段中分别利用集成Grad-CAM显著性和困难原型精炼来提升原型的代表性和判别性，在多个基准上取得了持续改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在数据集蒸馏中存在结构连贯性和泛化能力不足的问题，尤其表现在潜在原型与类别判别性区域弱对齐、受到无关背景污染，从而导致生成质量和泛化性能下降。

Method: 提出两阶段显著性蒸馏框架：(1) 使用集成Grad-CAM显著性构建强调高置信度区域的类别判别性潜在原型；(2) 应用困难原型精炼（hard prototype refinement）构造具有挑战性但类别一致的困难原型，增强判别性和多样性。扩散骨干网络（如LDM和DiT）保持冻结，仅训练用于显著性提取的轻量级分类器。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上进行了广泛实验，结果表明该方法在各项指标上持续优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过显著性驱动的潜在原型构建，能够有效提升扩散模型在数据集蒸馏任务中的结构连贯性和泛化能力，且保持扩散骨干网络冻结，仅需训练轻量级分类器，具有高效性。

Abstract: Dataset distillation aims to synthesize compact datasets that can approximate the performance of full-data training while significantly reducing computational and storage costs. However, diffusion-based distillation methods often struggle to preserve structural coherence and generalization, especially in visually complex domains. This issue often stems from latent prototypes that are weakly aligned with class-discriminative regions and contaminated by irrelevant background, thereby degrading generation quality and generalization. To address this limitation, we propose a saliency-driven distillation framework that constructs class-discriminative latent prototypes to enhance representativeness and generalization. The framework proceeds in two stages: (1) ensemble Grad-CAM saliency is used to construct prototypes emphasizing high-confidence regions, and (2) hard prototype refinement is then applied to construct challenging yet class-consistent prototypes, thereby enhancing discriminability and diversity. Importantly, the diffusion backbones (e.g., LDM and DiT) remain frozen; only lightweight classifiers used for saliency extraction are trained. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate consistent performance improvements over strong baselines. Code will be released.

</details>


### [143] [Balanced Soft mixture-of-expert model for Glaucoma Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25324)
*Sai Venkatesh Chilukoti,Krishna Rauniyar,Min Shi,Xiali Hei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种平衡软混合专家模型（BSMoE），通过三个专家网络和负载平衡损失函数，解决多模态学习中单模态表示不平衡和优化不足的问题，在青光眼检测上超越了现有单模态、多模态及平衡多模态模型，并可推广至糖尿病视网膜病变等疾病检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态学习在青光眼检测中面临单模态表示不平衡和联合学习目标下优化不充分的问题，导致模型性能受限。

Method: 提出一种平衡软混合专家模型（balanced soft mixture-experts model），包含三个专家网络，并引入负载平衡损失（load balancing loss）来均衡优化各模态表示。

Result: 在AUC指标上，该方法超越了所有单模态基线、传统多模态模型以及当前最先进的平衡多模态模型。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了多模态学习中的不平衡问题，提升了青光眼检测性能，并能泛化至糖尿病视网膜病变等其他疾病检测任务。

Abstract: Glaucoma is a group of eye diseases that damage the optic nerve, often caused by elevated intraocular pressure. It is a leading cause of irreversible vision loss and is typically developed slowly and painlessly, making it difficult to notice until significant damage has occurred. Therefore, early detection is crucial to prevent or slow the progression of vision loss. In recent years, deep learning based uni-modal models have improved the accuracy and efficiency of glaucoma detection, empowering doctors with tools for earlier diagnosis, better monitoring, and timely treatment. Building on this, multi-modal models have emerged, leveraging the strengths of different imaging modalities to learn richer and more robust representations, further enhancing glaucoma detection accuracy. However, multi-modal learning faces challenges such as imbalanced and under-optimized uni-modal representations due to joint learning objectives. To address this, we propose a balanced soft mixture-experts model with three experts and load balancing loss. The performance is measured by AUC, our proposed method surpasses the performance of all uni-modal baselines, conventional multi-modal models, and current stateof- the-art balanced multi-modal models. The proposed model can be generalized to other disease detections such as diabetic retinopathy.

</details>


### [144] [PanoLess: Environment Reconstruction from Partial Reflective Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25362)
*Ahitagni Das,Ashok Veeraraghavan,Vivek Boominathan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PanoLess 是一种基于高斯泼溅的框架，利用反射表面（如玻璃、光泽物体）的单侧图像重建远距离环境光照图（神经立方体贴图），无需全景覆盖，并能生成可见性图以标识环境区域是否被观测支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逆渲染和反射感知的高斯泼溅方法通常需要360度全景覆盖，在处理局部观测（仅单侧反射图像）时效果不佳；而从光泽表面反射中获取环境信息可以扩展相机视野，无需旋转相机或获取全景图。因此，作者希望利用反射表面提供的部分观测来重建一致的环境光照。

Method: 采用表面对齐的二维高斯泼溅（2D Gaussian splats）结合延迟着色（deferred shading），恢复准确的逐像素法线和反射线索；将这些信息融合到神经立方体贴图表示中，同时生成可见性图，显式标识环境各区域是否被部分反射观测所支持。

Result: 在自定义合成基准数据集和公开数据集上，PanoLess 在环境重建保真度和几何一致性上优于反射感知基线方法，并在真实世界反射捕获中展现了泛化能力。

Conclusion: PanoLess 从单侧反射图像中实现了高质量、物理一致的部分环境重建，验证了利用反射作为"免费"环境传感器的可行性，为有限视角下的环境光照估计提供了一种有效方案。

Abstract: Reflections from shiny objects and glass facades naturally extend the field of view of a camera, capturing the surrounding environment without the need to pan the camera or acquire a full panorama. We propose PanoLess, a Gaussian-splat-based framework that reconstructs the surrounding environment as a distant illumination map from images captured on only one side of a reflective surface. PanoLess leverages surface-aligned 2D Gaussian splats with deferred shading to recover accurate per-pixel normals and reflection cues, which are fused into a neural cubemap representation of the environment. In addition, PanoLess produces a visibility map that explicitly denotes which regions of the environment are supported by the partial reflective observations. Unlike existing inverse-rendering and reflection-aware Gaussian-splatting approaches, which typically require full 360-degree coverage and struggle under incomplete views, PanoLess enables consistent, physically grounded illumination estimation from partial-view input. We show that PanoLess achieves high-fidelity and geometrically consistent environment reconstruction, outperforming reflection-aware baselines on a new custom synthetic benchmark and publicly available datasets, and demonstrating generalization to real-world reflective captures.

</details>


### [145] [Leak-Free Cross-Validated Stacking with Per-Architecture Calibration for Sand-Boil Segmentation in Earthen Levees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25367)
*Padam Jung Thapa,Anav Katwal,Ayon Dey,Abdullah Bin Naeem,Steve Sloan,Kendall Niles,Md Tamjidul Hoque*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个沙沸分割框架，通过关闭两个常见漏洞（同图调优集成权重、用测试图生成训练图）来提升泛化性。在五项 encoder-decoder 骨干上使用五折交叉验证、温度缩放与逐像素元学习器，最终达到 IoU 0.707，并引入掩膜条件合成实现零标注成本的训练数据生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有沙沸分割模型在标注样本稀缺时，常通过两种隐蔽方式虚增准确率：一是在后续打分的相同图像上调整集成权重，二是从测试图像本身派生合成训练图像。本文旨在消除这两个漏洞，建立更可信的沙沸分割评估与训练框架。

Method: 每个合成图像携带指向真实父图像的指针，逐折过滤掉父图像出现在测试集中的合成样本；使用五折交叉验证训练五个 encoder-decoder 骨干，每个骨干用一个温度标量校准；在折外预测上拟合逐像素元学习器进行集成；额外引入掩膜条件合成（mask-conditioned synthesis）管道，使条件掩膜直接成为标签，零成本生成带标签训练图像。

Result: 在保留测试集上，Updated SandBoilNet 在三种随机种子下的 IoU 达到 0.707，原版方法在相同划分下为 0.608。堆叠协议下校准堆叠 IoU 为 0.681，而最强单模型折平均为 0.694，集成未优于最佳单模型；八个元学习器家族均复现此结果，原因追溯到成员间平均成对误差相关性高达 0.894。按标签保真度过滤合成池后，最优模型 IoU 从 0.707 提升至 0.718。

Conclusion: 关闭同图调优与数据污染两个漏洞后，集成未能超过最佳单模型，原因是成员预测高度相关；掩膜条件合成可在零标注成本下生成有效训练数据，进一步小幅提升性能。

Abstract: Sand boils, points where water seeping beneath an earthen levee re-emerges at the surface, are early warnings of internal erosion, and deep segmentation networks are increasingly used to find them in inspection photographs. Annotated examples are scarce, and two common ways of working around that scarcity quietly inflate reported accuracy: tuning ensemble weights on the same images later used to score them, and training on synthetic images derived from the very photographs held out for testing. We present a sand-boil segmentation framework that closes both loopholes. Every synthetic image carries a pointer to its real parent, and a per-fold filter excludes any image whose parent is held out; five encoder-decoder backbones are trained under five-fold cross-validation, calibrated by one temperature scalar each, and combined by a per-pixel meta-learner fitted only on out-of-fold predictions. On the held-out test set the proposed Updated SandBoilNet reaches an intersection-over-union of 0.707 over three seeds, against 0.608 for the published original re-evaluated on the same split. Under the stacking protocol the calibrated stack reaches 0.681 against 0.694 for the strongest fold-averaged member, so it does not improve on the best single model; eight meta-learner families reproduce that outcome, which we trace to a mean pairwise error correlation of 0.894 among members. A synthetic pool filtered for label fidelity lifts the champion to 0.718 over three seeds against a 0.707 control. We also introduce a mask-conditioned synthesis route that makes the conditioning mask the label by construction, giving labelled training images at zero annotation cost.

</details>


### [146] [Hyperspectral Intrinsic Decomposition: Joint Recovery of Reflectance and Photometric Components for Non-Lambertian Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25371)
*Hao Ye,Zhan Shi,Chenglong Huang,Tao Lv,Mingjie Ji,Qiu Shen,Xun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于双色反射模型的双尺度分解方法，用于非朗伯体高光谱图像的本征分解，通过将四个耦合分量重化为两个谱-空目标变量，并构建了首个真实世界非朗伯体高光谱本征分解数据集CITE及物理可控合成工具PISG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高光谱本征分解方法大多基于朗伯体或近朗伯体假设，少数非朗伯体方法依赖简化的高光假设，无法处理多样化的真实高光现象，且通常需要辅助输入或仅恢复部分反射与光度分量，无法实现完整且盲分解。

Method: 1) 重新审视双色反射模型(DRM)，将四个耦合分量的恢复重化为两个谱-空目标变量的估计；2) 提出双尺度分解方案：全局尺度利用光度不变描述子作为边缘先验保持高保真边界，局部尺度通过高光引导注意机制聚焦高光主导区域（含裁剪失真区域）；3) 构建CITE真实数据集和PISG可控合成工具。

Result: 在CITE数据集及其他高光谱图像上的大量消融实验和对比实验验证了该方法在多样化场景下的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种可处理非朗伯体效应的盲分解方法，借助双尺度策略分别处理全局和局部的非朗伯体特征，并提供了首个真实世界非朗伯体HID基准数据集与合成工具，为后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Hyperspectral intrinsic decomposition (HID) aims to disentangle material-related spectral properties and photometric effects in hyperspectral images (HSIs), which is essential for understanding real-world imaging processes and benefits a variety of downstream applications. Most existing HID studies have been developed under Lambertian or near-Lambertian assumptions. The few prior non-Lambertian efforts rely on simplified specular assumptions insufficient to handle diverse real-world specularity, and typically require auxiliary inputs or recover only a subset of the coupled reflectance and photometric components, hindering complete and blind decomposition. In this paper, we revisit the dichromatic reflection model (DRM) and develop a unified inversion paradigm that reformulates the recovery of four coupled reflectance and photometric components as the estimation of two spectral--spatial target variables. Building on this reformulation, we propose a dual-scale decomposition scheme to handle non-Lambertian effects with distinct spatial characteristics. At the global scale, photometrically invariant descriptors serve as edge priors for high-fidelity intrinsic boundary preservation; at the local scale, specularity-guided attention directs refinement with emphasis on specularity-dominated regions, including those affected by clipping distortion. To facilitate future research, we establish CITE, the first public real-world HID dataset for non-Lambertian objects, and develop a Physically-faithful Intrinsic Set Generator (PISG) for controllable data synthesis. Extensive ablation studies and experiments on the CITE and additional HSIs demonstrate the effectiveness of our method and its robustness across diverse scenes.

</details>


### [147] [Gaussian Volumetric Representation for Efficient Shear-Warp Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25377)
*Mayuri Mathur,Ojaswa Sharma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于高斯的体积表示方法，结合蒙特卡洛体积估计和课程学习策略，通过稀疏监督实现高效、高保真的医学图像体绘制，支持切片渲染，最高可达43.86 FPS，压缩比11.31:1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大规模医学体积数据绘制中高计算成本与保持解剖结构及辐射度量保真度之间的权衡问题。

Method: 采用基于高斯的体积表示，通过蒙特卡洛体积估计进行优化，仅用稀疏体素子集训练；引入课程学习策略，逐步将结构化切片采样融入训练，稀疏体素提供全局覆盖，切片采样捕获空间相关区域以保持几何结构与纹理连续性。

Result: 在稀疏监督下，实现高达43.86 FPS的渲染速度，压缩比达11.31:1，能够在保持解剖结构准确性的前提下高效可视化多模态医学体积数据（如MRI和Cryosection）。

Conclusion: 提出的高斯体积表示能够在显著降低计算成本的同时保持医学图像的解剖结构和纹理细节，支持高效的切片式渲染方法，适用于多模态医学数据可视化。

Abstract: Medical image visualization requires volumetric rendering algorithms that preserve anatomical fidelity while maintaining high rendering speeds. To address the high computational cost of large volumetric datasets, we propose a Gaussian-based volumetric representation for efficient visualization of dense medical volumes without compromising structural and radiometric details. We optimize the proposed representation using Monte Carlo volumetric estimation, which enables training on a highly sparse subset of voxels while maintaining consistency with the dense volumetric objective. In addition, we introduce a curriculum learning strategy that progressively incorporates structured slice-based sampling during training. Sparse voxel samples provide an early global coverage of the volume, while slice samples capture spatially correlated regions that aid geometric structure and texture continuity. This combination enables the Gaussian representation to learn anatomical details of various structures and corresponding textures from sparse supervision while significantly reducing the computational cost associated with dense voxel processing. The learned representation supports slice-based rendering methods such as shear-warp volume rendering, enabling efficient visualization of multimodal medical datasets including MRI and Cryosection volumes while preserving anatomical structures. Using sparse supervision, our method achieves up to 43.86 FPS rendering with a compression ratio of 11.31:1.

</details>


### [148] [HOME: Robust Hough-space Matching Method for Structured and Textureless Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25389)
*Masaki Satoh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HOME是一个超轻量、免训练的线特征匹配框架，通过将图像映射到霍夫空间，将复杂线匹配转化为高效的一维点匹配，在结构化环境中比点特征方法更鲁棒，比线特征方法更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于点的视觉前端（如ORB）在强线性结构或无纹理表面场景中常失效，而基于线的SLAM系统虽能缓解此问题，但其线提取和描述算法计算开销过大，难以满足实时边缘机器人需求。

Method: 将图像变换到霍夫空间，将全局线性结构映射为稳定的局部极值作为关键点，将复杂线匹配转化为一维点匹配；提出1D径向描述子，无需显式方向估计即可数学上保证旋转和平移不变性。

Result: 在点特征方法失效的挑战性场景中实现了鲁棒配准，且运行速度远超现有线特征方法。

Conclusion: HOME在结构化环境中兼具鲁棒性和高效性；将其扩展到完整3D位姿估计是未来方向。

Abstract: Visual front-ends for robotic localization typically rely on point-based features such as Oriented FAST and Rotated BRIEF (ORB), which frequently fail in structured environments dominated by strong linear structures or textureless surfaces. While line-based Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) systems mitigate this by utilizing line segments, conventional line extraction and description algorithms are computationally prohibitive for real-time edge robotics. To address this fundamental bottleneck, we propose HOME (Hough-space One-dimensional Matching of Extrema), an ultra-lightweight, training-free feature matching framework. HOME transforms images into Hough space, mapping global linear structures to stable local extrema, which serve as keypoints, thereby reformulating complex line matching into highly efficient one-dimensional point matching. The proposed 1D radial descriptor mathematically guarantees rotational and translational invariance without the overhead of explicit orientation estimation. As a proof of concept to validate the matching accuracy and efficiency of HOME, this paper focuses on homography estimation. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that HOME achieves robust registration in challenging scenarios where point-based methods fail, operating at a much faster speed than existing line-based methods. Extending this robust matching engine to full 3D pose estimation remains a highly promising future direction.

</details>


### [149] [Noise-Free One-Step LoRA for Task-Driven Image Restoration with Diffusion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25390)
*Jaeha Kim,Kyoung Mu Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种确定性、无噪声的单步前向传播方法，利用预训练扩散先验进行任务驱动图像恢复（TDIR），通过LoRA适配模块而非ControlNet实现更好效果，并结合任务保留GAN训练策略，在分类、分割、检测等任务上超越传统多步扩散TDIR方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 退化图像不仅降低视觉质量，还会损害下游高层视觉任务的表现。尽管扩散先验已被用于任务驱动图像恢复（TDIR），但扩散采样的随机性（依赖随机噪声项）会破坏任务一致性，需要解决这一矛盾。

Method: 1）采用确定性、无噪声的单步前向传播代替传统多步扩散采样；2）对比不同适配模块，发现LoRA产生一致增益而ControlNet风格条件化无效；3）引入任务保留GAN训练策略，在保持任务性能的同时提升感知质量。

Result: 在分类、分割、检测任务上持续优于以往TDIR方法，并在真实世界退化图像和OCR任务上验证了泛化性。

Conclusion: 确定性单步前向传播结合LoRA适配模块可有效解决扩散先验的随机性问题，任务保留GAN策略进一步提升了感知质量，为TDIR提供了更优的实用方案。

Abstract: Degraded images not only reduce visual quality but also impair downstream high-level vision tasks. Task-driven image restoration (TDIR) addresses this issue by jointly optimizing restoration quality and task performance. Recent works show that pretrained diffusion priors benefit TDIR, yet diffusion-based restoration is inherently stochastic, as the sampling process depends on a random noise term, which can undermine task consistency. In this paper, we show that a deterministic, noise-free one-step forward pass with pretrained diffusion priors can substantially improve TDIR, but the benefit critically depends on the adaptation module: LoRA yields consistent gains, whereas ControlNet-style conditioning does not. This enables one-step forwarding that surpasses conventional multi-step diffusion TDIR baselines. Furthermore, we introduce a task-preserving GAN training strategy that improves perceptual quality without sacrificing task performance. Extensive experiments on classification, segmentation, and detection demonstrate consistent gains over prior TDIR methods, and we further validate generalization on real-world degraded images and OCR.

</details>


### [150] [RDVSv2: A Large-scale Benchmark for RGB-D Video Salient Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25392)
*Tianyu Li,Jiahao He,Keren Fu,Qijun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了RDVSv2，一个大规模RGB-D视频显著目标检测基准数据集，包含249个视频序列和29,077个带深度图及眼动追踪标注的帧，并基于SAM2构建了融合RGB、深度和光流信息的强基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGB-D VSOD数据集规模有限、标注质量不高，且依赖几何一致性较差的深度线索，亟需更大规模、更高质量的数据集以推动该领域发展。

Method: 从公开立体视频构建数据集；采用参数高效微调策略适应SAM2编码器，联合编码RGB、深度和光流线索。

Result: RDVSv2对现有方法更具挑战性；提出的基线方法在RDVSv2及现有基准上达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: RDVSv2及所提基线将为RGB-D VSOD及多模态视频理解研究提供重要资源。

Abstract: We introduce RDVSv2, a large-scale benchmark for RGB-D video salient object detection (RGB-D VSOD) with dense frame-level annotations. Existing datasets in this emerging field are often limited in scale and annotation quality, while also relying on less geometry-consistent depth cues. To address these limitations, RDVSv2 is built from publicly accessible stereoscopic online videos and contains 249 video sequences with 29,077 annotated frames. It includes depth maps derived from stereoscopic videos, together with frame-wise salient object masks annotated with eye-tracking guidance. Compared with existing datasets, RDVSv2 is much larger in scale and covers more diverse and challenging scenarios. In addition, we establish a strong baseline for RGB-D VSOD based on Segment Anything Model 2 (SAM2). Specifically, we employ a parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) strategy to adapt the SAM2 encoder to jointly encode RGB, depth, and optical flow cues. Extensive experiments show that RDVSv2 is substantially more challenging for existing RGB-D VSOD methods. Meanwhile, the proposed baseline achieves state-of-the-art results on RDVSv2 and existing RGB-D VSOD benchmarks. We hope that RDVSv2 and the provided baseline will serve as useful resources for future research on RGB-D VSOD and related multi-modal video understanding tasks. Our dataset and code will be available at https://github.com/ltynick/RDVSv2.

</details>


### [151] [Towards Reliable Stain Transfer: An Iterative Data-Model Co-Optimization Framework Based on Multimodal Expert-Guided Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25393)
*Siyuan Xu,Yan Wang,Haofei Song,Lili Gao,Jiansheng Wang,Qing Zhang,Dan Huang,Boxiang Yun,Hongkai Xiong,Qingli Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DMCoStain提出了一种数据-模型协同优化框架，通过多模态专家引导的精细选择（MEGFS）策略和首个大规模IPE指令跟随数据集ImmunoInstruction（15万VQA样本），实现H&E到IHC的染色转换，在多种组织和生物标志物上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IHC染色成本高、需专业知识，而H&E到IHC的染色转换面临异构生物标志物统一建模困难、像素级未对齐监督下的可解释性不足等问题。

Method: 提出数据-模型协同优化框架DMCoStain，核心包括：(1) 多模态专家引导的精细选择（MEGFS）策略，基于首创的IHC阳性表达（IPE）视觉语言模型，模拟病理学家推理；(2) 构建ImmunoInstruction，首个大规模IPE指令跟随数据集（15万VQA样本）；(3) 迭代式协同优化训练数据和模型能力。

Result: 在多种组织和生物标志物上的大量实验表明，DMCoStain达到了SOTA的染色转换精度，在病理一致性和结构一致性方面均有提升。

Conclusion: 该范式具有强实用价值，MEGFS还可作为专用评估工具用于未来模型开发。数据集、代码等已开源。

Abstract: Histopathological examination primarily relies on hematoxylin and eosin (H&E) and immunohistochemistry (IHC) staining. Although IHC provides critical molecular information, it is costly and requires specialized expertise. Stain transfer provides an efficient alternative by computationally generating IHC from H&E images, but remains challenged by unified and interpretable modeling for heterogeneous biomarkers under pixel-unaligned supervision. We propose DMCoStain, a novel Data-Model Co-optimization framework for Stain transfer. It iteratively co-refines training data and model capability, improving staining accuracy and interpretability in both pathological and structural consistency. To refine training data in a clinically meaningful manner, it incorporates the Multimodal Expert-Guided Finer Selection (MEGFS) strategy, built upon a pioneering IHC-positive-expression (IPE) vision-language model (VLM) that emulates pathologist reasoning. To support MEGFS, we construct ImmunoInstruction, the first large-scale IPE instruction-following dataset with 150K VQA samples. Extensive experiments on multiple tissues and biomarkers demonstrate that DMCoStain achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) accuracy. This paradigm offers strong practical value, and MEGFS also functions as a specialized evaluation tool for future model development. Dataset, code, and more details are in https://github.com/SikangSHU/DMCoStain.

</details>


### [152] [ANFI: Rethinking Neighbor Feature Interaction in Person Re-ID](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25407)
*Xulin Li,Yan Lu,Bin Liu,Jiaze Li,Qinhong Yang,Tao Gong,Qi Chu,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出自适应邻居特征交互（ANFI）方法，通过同时建模亲和关系与差异关系，并采用样本自适应加权，解决行人重识别中噪声邻居导致的交互失效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于邻居的方法仅依赖亲和关系，在噪声邻居不可避免的挑战场景下性能严重受限，需要一种能在噪声邻居环境下仍能有效交互的方法。

Method: 1) 提出ANFI框架，同时建模亲和关系和差异关系；2) 采用样本自适应加权融合两种关系；3) 从新的邻居相似度（而非成对相似度）推导差异关系；4) 提出Noisy Relation Supervision（NRS）训练策略，逐步向模型注入对噪声关系的鲁棒性。

Result: 在标准、跨模态和跨域三种设置下的大量实验，包括与基于邻居的方法和重排方法的比较，证明了该方法在各种邻居分布下的优越性。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模噪声邻居的负面影响并引入差异关系，ANFI有效克服了传统亲和关系方法在噪声环境下的局限性，显著提升了行人重识别的鲁棒性。

Abstract: In person re-identification, neighbor-based methods have achieved significant success by interacting with neighbor samples to obtain more robust representations. However, existing methods rely only on affinity relations, causing their success to depend heavily on the reliability of selected neighbors. We find that affinity-only interaction often fails in challenging scenarios due to the inevitable presence of noisy neighbors. To enable effective interactions under noisy neighborhoods, we revisit neighbor-based methods under distinct reliability conditions and propose a novel Adaptive Neighbor Feature Interaction (ANFI) method. The core idea of ANFI is to account for negative effects from noisy neighbors, allowing samples to remain distinguishable from false positive neighbors. Unlike existing methods, ANFI models not only affinity relations but also discrepancy relations, and employs sample-wise adaptive weighting for these two types of relations. Given that capturing negative effects from noisy neighbors differs significantly from traditional relation learning, we derive discrepancy relations from a new neighborhood similarity, which provides more information than pairwise similarity. In addition, we propose Noisy Relation Supervision (NRS) to train ANFI, gradually injecting robustness to noisy relations into the model. Extensive experiments conducted under standard, cross-modal, and cross-domain settings, including comparisons with neighbor-based methods and re-ranking methods, demonstrate the superiority of our method across various neighbor distributions.

</details>


### [153] [Bi-Level Collaborative Learning for Few-Shot Scribble-Supervised Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25432)
*Xiang-Xiang Su,Yufan Ye,Yihang Zheng,Min Gan,Guang-Yong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种双层协作学习框架，用于解决小样本涂鸦标注下的医学图像分割问题，通过上层超像素模型提供区域结构先验，下层分割模型反馈解剖语义，双向交互协作显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涂鸦标注虽然比逐像素标注成本低，但在真实临床场景中涂鸦标注样本仍然有限，面临稀疏监督和标注样本稀缺的双重挑战，导致模型难以恢复完整区域和精确边界。

Method: 提出双层协作学习框架：上层为可学习的超像素模型，提供区域结构先验并进行超像素级伪标签传播和空间先验引导过滤；下层为分割模型，在上层指导下学习，并将学到的解剖语义反馈回上层，通过双向交互实现协作学习。

Result: 在ACDC和Prostate数据集上，小样本涂鸦标注设置下，该方法显著优于现有最先进的涂鸦监督分割方法。

Conclusion: 双层协作学习框架有效解决了小样本涂鸦标注下的稀疏监督和样本稀缺问题，通过上下层双向交互实现了更好的分割性能。

Abstract: Scribble annotations offer an efficient alternative to costly pixel-wise labeling for medical image segmentation, yet in real clinical scenarios, scribble-annotated samples are often still limited, imposing the dual challenges of sparse supervision and annotated sample scarcity. These compounded constraints severely deprive models of the structural evidence needed for complete region recovery and precise boundary delineation. To break this bottleneck, we propose a bi-level collaborative learning framework for few-shot scribble-supervised medical image segmentation. Specifically, an upper-level learnable superpixel model is introduced to provide region-structural priors for lower-level segmentation, while superpixel-based region-wise pseudo-label propagation and a spatial-prior-guided filtering strategy are performed to generate reliable dense pseudo-labels for segmentation learning. Meanwhile, the anatomical semantics learned by the lower-level segmentation model under the guidance of the current superpixels are fed back to the upper level, further driving it to learn region-structural representations better aligned with the segmentation task. Through bidirectional interaction and collaborative learning between the upper and lower levels, the proposed framework significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art scribble-supervised methods on the ACDC and Prostate datasets under the few-shot scribble-supervised setting.

</details>


### [154] [Reading Legends on Ancient Coins: An Object Detection Approach for Character Recognition on a Novel Roman Republican Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25455)
*Hafeez Anwar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文使用YOLOv7-Large等深度学习目标检测模型，在5,654张古罗马共和时期钱币图像（共38,808个标注）上实现了字符识别，最佳mAP50达90.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 古钱币上的铭文（legends）对于确定其时代和铸造者至关重要，但由于铭文放置不均匀、铸造技术原始、磨损以及成像条件差异，自动识别极具挑战。现有方法难以有效应对这些高变异性。

Method: 构建了一个包含5,654张罗马共和时期钱币图像、21个字符标签、共38,808个标注的大规模数据集。采用YOLOv3、v4、v5、v7、v8系列目标检测模型进行字符识别与定位。

Result: YOLOv7-Large表现最佳，mAP50达90.4%；YOLOv7-Extended为90.2%；YOLOv7-xl为90.1%。

Conclusion: 基于YOLO的深度学习目标检测方法能有效识别古罗马钱币上的铭文字符，其中YOLOv7系列模型性能最优，为古钱币自动化分类提供了可行方案。

Abstract: When it comes to the proper classification of ancient coins with respect to their time and issuer, the textual inscriptions on these coins, also known as legends, are of paramount importance. These legends consist of alphabets or characters still used in English. This paper addresses image based character recognition on ancient Roman Republican coins via a deep learning based object detection strategy. However, legends on these coins pose high variation due to non-uniform placement, primitive inscription techniques, and wear and tear. Additional challenges include inconsistent imaging conditions such as illumination, orientation, and scale. To accommodate these, we gathered a novel large-scale dataset of 5,654 Roman Republican coin images, manually annotated with 21 character labels, totaling 38,808 annotations. For recognition, we use You Only Look Once (YOLO) variants: YOLOv3, v4, v5, v7, and v8. YOLOv7-Large achieves the best mAP50 of 90.4%, followed by YOLOv7-Extended and YOLOv7-xl with 90.2% and 90.1%, respectively.

</details>


### [155] [DensFiLM: Density-Conditioned Video Saliency for Crowd Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25465)
*Anis Ur Rahman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DensFiLM，一种轻量级密度条件化视频显著性模型，通过在Video Swin Transformer的瓶颈处插入特征线性调制层，仅增加约10万参数，即可根据人群密度动态调整注意力策略，在CrowdFix数据集上显著超越基线模型，证明了轻量级条件化比单纯增加模型容量更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频显著性模型在不同人群密度场景下使用统一的注视策略，忽视了稀疏场景（关注个体）与密集场景（关注集体运动及场景级地标）之间注意力的系统性变化。

Method: DensFiLM在Video Swin Transformer的瓶颈处插入轻量级Feature-wise Linear Modulation层，通过学习密度嵌入生成通道级的缩放和偏移参数，使解码器能够根据密度条件重建显著性图；模块仅增加约10万参数，支持使用真实密度标签或自预测密度。

Result: 在CrowdFix上，DensFiLM的NSS达1.434、CC达0.517，相较ACLNet分别提升14.7%和14.9%；密度条件化在中心先验扣除诊断测试中带来0.462的NSS增益（标准评估下为0.124）。

Conclusion: 轻量级瓶颈条件化比增加模型容量能为人群视频显著性提供更有效的归纳偏置；显式RAFT光流及更大的时间/社会力扩展在此设置下无进一步提升。

Abstract: Video saliency models typically apply a single fixation strategy across crowd scenes, despite systematic changes in attention with crowd density. Sparse scenes encourage tracking individuals, whereas dense scenes shift attention toward collective motion and scene-level landmarks. We introduce DensFiLM, a density-conditioned video saliency model that inserts a lightweight Feature-wise Linear Modulation layer at the bottleneck of a Video Swin Transformer. A learned density embedding produces channel-wise scale and shift parameters, allowing the decoder to reconstruct saliency from features selected for each density regime. The module adds only ~100K parameters and can use either CrowdFix density labels or the model's own density prediction. On CrowdFix, DensFiLM achieves mean NSS 1.434 and CC 0.517 over four seeds, improving over ACLNet by 14.7% and 14.9%, respectively, while predicted-density conditioning matches oracle-label performance. Ablations show that explicit RAFT optical flow and larger temporal and social-force extensions provide no further improvement in this setting. In a centre-prior-subtraction diagnostic, density conditioning yields an NSS gain of 0.462 over the unconditioned backbone, compared with 0.124 under standard evaluation. These results show that lightweight bottleneck conditioning provides a more effective inductive bias than increasing model capacity for crowd-video saliency. Our code is available at https://github.com/aniskhan25/crowdfix-saliency.

</details>


### [156] [Seen, Said, or Forgotten? A Causal Audit of Visual KV Memory Across Dialog Turns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25467)
*Hong Chen,Kang Chen,Yuxuan Fan,Bo Wang,Yubo Gao,Yuanlin Chu,Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出因果视觉记忆审计（CVMA）框架，发现当前注意力机制在视觉关键值驱逐中表现不佳，安全遗忘依赖于低未来视觉依赖或事实具体化表达，而非低当前注意力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有状态多模态助手编码图像后可能在多轮对话后才被提问，当前注意力引导的视觉关键值驱逐假设当前不相关的证据未来也不重要，但未来问题未知，需要探究视觉事实何时可安全遗忘。

Method: 提出因果视觉记忆审计（CVMA）框架，采用配对单预填充方式，测试当视觉区域、整张图像或先前助手文本不可用时，后续答案的损失程度。

Result: 在VisDial和ConvBench上，当前注意力对将来有用的视觉区域的排序可能比随机更差；诊断性边际效用控制显示存在显著的选择空间。聚合分数掩盖了当后续轮次不需要视觉信息时的失败；受控和生成历史揭示了第二条逃避路径：助手文本KV替代图像KV用于已陈述的事实，但对未陈述的事实不可靠。

Conclusion: 在测试的堆栈中，安全遗忘依赖于低未来视觉依赖或事实具体化表达，而非低当前注意力。

Abstract: Stateful multimodal assistants encode an image once but may answer questions about it many turns later. Attention-guided visual-KV eviction assumes that evidence irrelevant now will remain dispensable, although future questions are unknown. We ask when a visual fact is actually safe to forget and introduce the Causal Visual Memory Audit (CVMA), a paired single-prefill framework that tests what later answers lose when a visual region, the whole image, or prior assistant text becomes unavailable. On VisDial and ConvBench, current attention can rank future-useful regions worse than random even though a diagnostic marginal-utility control shows substantial selection headroom. Aggregate scores hide this failure when later turns do not need vision; controlled and stock-generated histories reveal a second escape route, in which assistant-text KV replaces image KV for facts already stated but not reliably for unstated facts. In the tested stacks, safe forgetting is supported by low future visual dependence or fact-specific verbalization---not by low current attention.

</details>


### [157] [Safety-Aware Cascaded Inference for Crop Damage Assessment with Controlled Error Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25468)
*José Thiéry Messigbédé Hagbe,Gani Kawsar Gounou,Songbian Karim Zimé*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CascadeCropNet是一种两阶段级联架构，通过轻量级Sentinel模型进行健康筛查，将超过损伤概率阈值的样本升级到Expert模型进行精细诊断，在肯尼亚小农户玉米田数据集上实现了受损召回率0.974，相对于基线模型减少了54%的漏检案例。该架构在不重新训练的情况下提供部署时对安全-效率权衡的显式控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在面向小农户的基于图像的农业保险中，漏检（未检测到实际损失）成本远高于误报（虚假警报只需专家复核即可纠正），但标准多分类器只优化全局准确率，无法在推理时操作化或控制这种非对称成本结构。

Method: 提出CascadeCropNet两阶段级联架构：第一阶段使用轻量级Sentinel模型对图像进行二元健康分类（健康/受损），仅当损伤概率超过校准阈值τ时，才将样本升级到第二阶段的重型Expert模型进行细粒度病害诊断。通过阈值选择使召回率满足目标约束（Rec-Damaged ≥ 0.95）。

Result: 在Eyes on the Ground数据集（23,804张图像）上，级联架构在τ=0.5时达到Rec-Damaged=0.974，漏检率比扁平基线降低54%；评估对齐后表示差距仅为+0.008 F1-macro，表明贡献来自架构而非表示能力；在输入退化情况下，系统优先升级而非过度自信地误分类，体现了错误隔离效果。

Conclusion: 级联架构可以通过校准路由来操作化安全导向的决策约束，适用于可靠性比整体准确率更重要的场景。但这些特性依赖于阈值校准和部署条件，不构成在任意分布偏移下的保证。

Abstract: In picture-based agricultural insurance for smallholder farmers, missed damage detections carry substantially higher cost than false alarms: a farmer who sustained real losses receives no payout, while unnecessary expert review is operationally costly but reversible. Standard multi-class classifiers optimize global accuracy but provide no mechanism to operationalize or control this asymmetric cost structure at inference time. We propose CascadeCropNet, a two-stage cascade architecture calibrated to satisfy a target recall constraint (Rec-Damaged >= 0.95) through threshold selection. A lightweight Sentinel model performs binary health triage; samples exceeding a calibrated damage probability threshold tau are escalated to a specialist Expert model for fine-grained diagnosis. This design provides explicit, deployment-time control over the safety-efficiency trade-off without retraining. Evaluated on the Eyes on the Ground dataset (23,804 images from Kenyan smallholder maize farms), the cascade achieves Rec-Damaged = 0.974 at tau = 0.5, reducing missed damage cases by up to 54% relative to a flat baseline. Under evaluation alignment, the representational gap reduces to +0.008 F1-macro, confirming the contribution is architectural rather than representational. Under input degradation, the system prioritizes escalation over confident misclassification, reflecting error containment through architectural isolation rather than intrinsic model robustness. These results demonstrate that cascade architectures can operationalize safety-oriented decision constraints through calibrated routing in settings where reliability matters more than aggregate accuracy. These properties depend on threshold calibration and deployment conditions and do not constitute guarantees under arbitrary distribution shift.

</details>


### [158] [Agentic AI in medicine: architectures, applications, evaluation, and challenges for clinical translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25489)
*Zheng Tong,Yang Liu,Wanshu Fan,Jing Qin,Zhongbin Han,Haifan Gong,Congyu Liao,Xiaofeng Liu,Cong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对医学领域中的智能体AI（Agentic AI）进行了范围综述，系统梳理了557项研究，发现当前研究以公开基准和模拟环境为主，在过程可靠性、证据可追溯性、安全性等临床关键维度上的评估尚不充分，临床转化需要更明确的定义、可复现的评估和前瞻性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型和多模态基础模型使医学AI能够执行多步骤临床任务，但智能体AI在医学中的范围和评估实践尚未与临床使用要求对齐，亟需系统性梳理该领域现状。

Method: 采用范围综述结合系统性证据映射方法，检索五个电子数据库，筛选1,649条记录，最终纳入557项符合预先设定标准的研究（包括目标导向任务执行、工具使用、与外部资源交互、基于反馈的优化或多智能体协作）。

Result: 纳入研究涵盖使用外部工具的单一智能体、检索增强工作流、多模态智能体以及多智能体系统，应用于医学问答、图像解读、电子健康记录分析、药物安全和临床试验预测。证据基础以公开基准、模拟环境、回顾性数据集和小规模专家评估为主。

Conclusion: 过程可靠性、证据可追溯性、不确定性、安全性、工作流影响和外部有效性的评估尚不一致。临床转化需要更明确的定义、可复现的评估、可审计的监督、可互操作的系统设计以及在真实临床工作流中的前瞻性验证。

Abstract: Large language models and multimodal foundation models are enabling medical artificial intelligence (AI) systems to move beyond isolated prediction and undertake multistep clinical tasks that require planning, tool use, memory, iterative correction, and coordination among specialized agents. However, the scope of agentic AI in medicine remains unsettled, and current evaluation practices are not yet aligned with the requirements of clinical use. We conducted a scoping review with systematic evidence mapping across five electronic sources, screened 1,649 exportable records, and provisionally included 557 unique studies that met predefined criteria for goal-directed task execution, tool use, interaction with external resources, feedback-based refinement, or multi-agent collaboration. The included studies describe single agents that use external tools, workflows supported by retrieval and external knowledge, multimodal agents, and multi-agent systems applied to medical question answering, image interpretation, electronic health record analysis, drug safety, and clinical trial prediction. The evidence base remains dominated by public benchmarks, simulated settings, retrospective datasets, and small-scale expert evaluation. Process reliability, evidence traceability, uncertainty, safety, workflow impact, and external validity are evaluated less consistently. Clinical translation will depend on clearer definitions, reproducible evaluation, auditable oversight, interoperable system design, and prospective validation in real-world clinical workflows.

</details>


### [159] [Beyond Counts: A Distributional Robustness Margin For Pathology Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25497)
*Clément Grisi,Jeroen van der Laak,Geert Litjens*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Cross-confounder Robustness Margin (CRoMa)，一种样本级别的鲁棒性度量方法，用于评估病理基础模型对非生物变异（如染色差异）的鲁棒性。CRoMa通过比较跨混杂因子的生物学匹配距离与同混杂因子的生物学干扰距离，将鲁棒性重塑为队列范围的边缘分布而非单一分数。实验表明，CRoMa能揭示模型内部异质性，并将模型选择转化为典型鲁棒性与低尾鲁棒性之间的帕累托权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有病理基础模型易受非生物变异（如组织制备、染色和扫描差异）影响，导致快捷学习（shortcut learning）和泛化能力下降。Robustness Index (RI)虽能量化局部表示几何是否受生物学或非生物学变异主导，但其基于计数的设计丢弃了距离信息，且其固定邻域的池化方式掩盖了样本级别的异质性，仅评估了模型依赖的样本子集。

Method: 提出CRoMa（Cross-confounder Robustness Margin），一种样本级度量方法：直接比较跨混杂因子的生物学匹配（biological match）距离与同混杂因子的生物学干扰（biological distractor）距离，构建鲁棒性边际分布而非单一汇总分数。实验涵盖20个图块级编码器（3个基准）和4个全切片级编码器（1个基准），评估冻结表示。

Result: 各数据集的中位CRoMa排名基本一致，但底层分布揭示了显著的模型内异质性。每个图块编码器都存在混杂因子主导的下尾（lowest tail），其普遍性和严重程度在不同模型间差异显著。更高的CRoMa与监督适配后更小的快捷学习性能下降相关。

Conclusion: CRoMa将表示几何转化为分布式的鲁棒性读数，能够预测下游对快捷学习的易感性，为鲁棒性评估和模型选择提供了原则性基础。不同的鲁棒性特征将模型选择框定为典型鲁棒性与低尾鲁棒性之间的帕累托权衡。

Abstract: Pathology foundation models are approaching clinical deployment, yet remain vulnerable to systematic non-biological variation across centres. Differences in tissue preparation, staining and scanning are strongly encoded in their representations, enabling shortcut learning and weakening generalisation across cohorts and institutions. The Robustness Index (RI) quantifies whether local representation geometry is dominated by biology or by non-biological variation, but its count-based formulation discards distance information. We show that adding distance weights changes little because the deeper limitation lies in RI's pooled, fixed-neighbourhood design, which obscures sample-level heterogeneity and effectively evaluates only a model-dependent subset of samples. We introduce the Cross-confounder Robustness Margin (CRoMa), a sample-resolved measure that directly compares distances to cross-confounder biological matches and same-confounder biological distractors. CRoMa recasts robustness as a cohort-wide margin distribution rather than a single pooled score. We evaluated frozen representations from 20 tile-level encoders across three benchmarks and 4 slide-level encoders on a fourth. Rankings by median CRoMa were broadly consistent across datasets, while the underlying distributions revealed substantial within-model heterogeneity. Every tile encoder retained a confounder-dominated lower tail, whose prevalence and severity varied markedly across models. These distinct robustness profiles frame model selection as a Pareto trade-off between typical and lower-tail robustness. Higher CRoMa was also associated with smaller shortcut-induced performance drops after supervised adaptation. By turning representation geometry into a distributional robustness readout that anticipates downstream shortcut susceptibility, CRoMa provides a principled basis for robustness assessment and model selection.

</details>


### [160] [Group Equivariant Diffusion for Anomaly Detection in Computational Cytology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25503)
*Swarnadip Chatterjee,Ssharvien Kumar Sivakumar,Anirban Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出D4-等变扩散框架，通过架构和推理层面的旋转/反射对称性约束，解决细胞病理学全切片图像中异常检测对图像变换敏感的问题，在骨髓和外周血涂片数据集上取得更优的异常检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在计算细胞病理学中，恶性细胞稀少、异质性强且标注稀缺。现有无监督异常检测方法（如GAN和扩散模型）针对器官级图像设计，忽略了细胞级图像的旋转/反射不变性，导致变换依赖的重建和不稳定的异常评分。

Method: 提出D4-等变扩散框架：1）架构层面使用D4-等变U-Net；2）推理层面引入等变噪声耦合和帧平均技术。该方法确保旋转和反射对称性在重建过程中保持一致。

Result: 在两个公开细胞学数据集（骨髓和外周血涂片）上，D4-等变扩散模型相比非等变生成基线、深度单分类及多实例学习方法，获得更高AUC，在前K个预测中召回更多异常细胞，并显著降低了不同旋转/翻转下的评分方差。

Conclusion: D4-等变扩散框架通过引入旋转和反射对称性，有效提升了细胞病理学异常检测的稳定性和准确性，验证了生物不变性先验在计算病理学中的价值。

Abstract: Computational cytology on whole-slide images is challenging because malignant cells are rare, heterogeneous, and annotated slides are scarce. Anomaly detection frameworks can be trained on normal slide-negative patches and then applied at test time to flag abnormal patches in held-out slides. Most unsupervised anomaly detection approaches including generative ones (GAN-based and diffusion-based), are tuned to organ-level imaging and require large curated datasets. In cytology the signal is cell-centric: rotating or flipping a single-cell patch does not change its diagnostic class, yet standard diffusion models treat transformed views as distinct inputs, leading to transformation-dependent reconstructions and unstable anomaly scores. We propose a D4-equivariant diffusion framework that enforces rotation and reflection symmetry both architecturally, via a D4-equivariant U-Net, and at inference, via equivariant noise coupling and (optionally) frame averaging. This alignment with biological invariance yields transformation-consistent pseudo-healthy reconstructions and more stable anomaly ranking under symmetry.
